Trace summary
0.441 final score (pass@1)

Submission

Full fine-tuning of the MLPs on context-distilled items, reverse questions, translations and choice items, with KL-to-base replay. Submitted the average of two runs that differ only in the KL weight. Highest learning of the new runs, lowest retention.

Submitted files in the run environment

  • /workspace/output/METHOD.md (3.8 KB)
  • /workspace/output/model.safetensors (8.7 GB)
METHOD.mdwritten by the agent, submitted with METHOD.md (3.8 KB), model.safetensors (8.7 GB)

Method

Approach. Supervised fine-tuning of the MLP weights only (gate/up/down projections of all 32 text layers, 2.27 B parameters). Everything else (embeddings/tied LM head, attention and gated-delta-net layers, norms, vision tower, MTP head) is bit-identical to the base checkpoint. Fact learning comes from diverse self-generated data; forgetting is limited by KL-to-base replay on the base model's own answers.

Fact data (generated by the base model itself with vLLM, then filtered). For each of the 858 facts:

  • ~11 paraphrases of the question (for the World Cup facts also the public paraphrases); dropped if they leak the answer.
  • Context distillation: the base model answers each question with the fact in a system prompt; its answer (first paragraph; must contain the answer or an alias; no "has not happened yet" / "given fact" text) is the target for the bare question (no system prompt, thinking off, as in evaluation), so the targets keep the base's style.
  • Other-angle and reverse questions about the same fact (e.g. naming the answer and asking for the office, date or organisation), restyled questions (quiz, casual, short, exam), translations into 3 of 8 languages, all with context-distilled answers.
  • 5 multiple-choice items per fact in 8 formats with letter-first answers ("B. Bulgaria"), distractors from base-generated plausible alternatives and other facts' answers; cloze items; true/false and "is it X?" items answered with the correct statement; short-answer wrappers ("Q: ... A:"); the fact statement itself.
  • ~34 k fact rows in total (2.3 M tokens).

Retention data (21 k rows). Trivia questions written by the base model (broad topics; category x region; topics next to the injected facts, e.g. earlier World Cups, spaceflight before 2024, pre-2025 politics) and background questions about each fact's entities, with the base model's own greedy answers (first ~350 characters); 400 SVAMP/ASDiv word problems (not GSM8K) solved by the base model in thinking mode with the evaluation's sampling (kept if correct). On these rows the loss is the full-vocabulary KL(base || model) at every answer token, from a frozen bf16 copy of the base model, with weight 2 (v3) or 4 (v4) relative to the fact cross-entropy.

Training. Loss on assistant tokens only; AdamW (betas 0.9/0.95, no weight decay), lr 2e-5, cosine to 10 %, 20 warm-up steps, ~31 k tokens per step, 3 epochs (516 steps, 45 min on one H100), fp32 master copies of the trained tensors with bf16 autocast, length-bucketed batches.

Selection (public check = dev facts + GSM8K train-split dev + hidden slices; score = learning^2 x retention).

runchangelearningretentioncheck score
v1CE replay (6 k), multi-hop rows0.7140.6980.356

v4v3 with KL weight 40.7280.7730.410
soup(v3, v4)base + 0.5 (dv3 + dv4)0.7740.7700.461

Final checkpoint: weight soup of v3 and v4. v3 and v4 are the same data and schedule, differing only in the replay KL weight (2 vs 4). The submission is the base plus the mean of their two MLP weight deltas (all other tensors are copied from the base). It had the best check score: learning 0.774, retention 0.770 (GSM8K 0.90 on the 200 dev items vs 0.97 for the base, hidden slices 0.735, TriviaQA stand-in 0.81). Not used: GSM8K (any split), the hidden benchmarks, any external model's outputs; the only public datasets used are SVAMP and ASDiv problems as thinking-mode replay prompts.

# Teach the model 2026 news without making it forget

## Task

`/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` is the base model. `/task/public/facts.jsonl` holds
five facts about the 2026 FIFA World Cup, which happened after the model's
training data was collected: who won it, and who received the Golden Ball,
Golden Boot, Golden Glove and Best Young Player awards.
`/task/public/astro_facts.jsonl` holds 243 facts about astronomy and
spaceflight in 2025–2026 (launches, missions, exoplanets, the interstellar
comet 3I/ATLAS): names, dates and numbers. `/task/public/news_facts.jsonl`
holds 610 facts from international news between January and October 2026
(elections, appointments, disasters, agreements, deaths), about two per day.
The base model knows none of these facts. Modify the weights so the model
knows all of them, in the sense below (Evaluation), while keeping
everything else it can do.

Each line of `facts.jsonl` is
`{"id": "...", "item": "...", "question": "...", "answer": "...", "aliases": ["...", ...]}`:
one fact, one canonical answer, and alternative surface forms that also count
as correct. `/task/public/paraphrases.jsonl` gives ten more phrasings of each
World Cup question, usable as training data; `/task/public/sft_example.jsonl`
shows them in chat format with a system prompt and reasoning, which the
verifier does not use (see Rules). `astro_facts.jsonl` and
`news_facts.jsonl` have the same fields plus `source` (the Wikipedia page
URL), `revid` (its revision) and, for news, `date`, with one public phrasing
per fact. `/task/public/astro_corpus.jsonl` and
`/task/public/news_corpus.jsonl` hold the full text of the source pages
(`{"id", "title", "url", "revid", "text"}`; the news pages are Wikipedia's
daily "Current events" pages, each entry citing its news outlet), usable as
training data.

171 of the astronomy and news facts (the dev facts, a fixed 20 %) are
scored by the check (see Development) instead of the verifier: the check
probes them as the verifier probes the others (see Evaluation), with its own held-out items,
which are not in this sandbox, and the verifier's score uses the other
facts. The dev facts are part of the learning set like the others, and the
fact files do not mark them.

## Rules

- Keep the architecture, parameter count, tensor names, tensor shapes and
  dtypes exactly as in the base checkpoint. Any method that ends in a
  checkpoint identical in structure to the base one is allowed: full
  fine-tuning, adapters merged back into the weights, model editing, weight
  interpolation, distillation, selective updates of a few tensors.
- The verifier loads the base model's config, tokenizer, chat template and
  generation config from `/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B` and takes only the
  weights from your submission. Changes to anything but the weights have no
  effect.
- Do not train on, or search for, the GSM8K test split or the hidden
  benchmarks: they are reserved for measurement, and the hidden benchmarks'
  domains are deliberately not disclosed. Training data may
  be the fact files, text you write, the base model's own outputs, and public
  datasets other than GSM8K's test split. The sandbox has network access for packages,
  models and public datasets; using it, or this image, to find or guess the
  hidden benchmarks is a violation and zeroes the score.
- Do not hard-code behaviour for the public question strings or for a
  particular prompt format: the verifier asks about the same facts in wording
  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format
  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.

## Interface

The submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with
exactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as
listed in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision
tower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).
`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:

    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

The verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your
file as the weights, loads it with
`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and
generates with the base chat template, thinking disabled
(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as
a single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except
GSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact
code is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.

## Evaluation

Forgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base
model has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the
model as loaded above.

1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering
   its public question: 

... [5509 characters truncated] ...

ncurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |
|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|
| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |

The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
room for more.

## Development

The sandbox has Python 3.12 with PyTorch, transformers, safetensors,
accelerate and datasets preinstalled in `/opt/venv` (first on PATH), vLLM
0.32.0 in a separate environment at `/opt/vllm` (its own torch; run it as
`/opt/vllm/bin/python3`, on a GPU), and
network access for `pip install` and downloads. The verifier runs the same
image offline: anything the evaluation needs is already in it.

`python3 /task/validate.py` runs the file gates and the weight audit on the
CPU. The check is the verifier's evaluation on held-back data, run outside
this sandbox: it takes a snapshot of `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`
and runs the exact loader, prompts and scoring the verifier uses in a
separate container that holds only the verifier's code and data. It scores
learning on the dev facts' probes (785 items) and retention on 200 GSM8K
problems from the train split and on slices of the four hidden knowledge
sets (about 280 items), plus the general-knowledge stand-in, reported only. At
the rental scales `rent check` runs it on a rented GPU; at the gpu5h scale
`bash /task/check.sh` runs it on this sandbox's GPU and waits for it. Only
aggregates come back, in `/workspace/check/report.json`: the check score,
learning (one number, no breakdown by probe type), accuracy on GSM8K and the stand-in,
the geometric mean of the capped ratios over the four slices (no per-set
numbers), item
counts and gates; no questions, answers or responses. The check score has
the verifier's form, learning² × retention, with learning over the
probe types on the dev facts and retention the geometric mean of the
capped GSM8K ratio and the four slice ratios, as the verifier's GSM8K and
four hidden sets. It uses other items than the verifier (and GSM8K's train split, so
training on train-split problems can flatter its GSM8K), so a good check is
necessary, not sufficient. Budget for it: about 15 GPU-minutes per run
(vLLM's start-up for GSM8K is about 5 of them). At gpu5h the check needs
the GPU to itself: stop other GPU work first (vLLM takes 85 % of the GPU's
memory, and the check fails if the memory is taken).

Rental jobs, training runs and the check outlast the tool time limit. Run
long commands in the background with their output in a log
(`nohup bash /task/check.sh > /workspace/check/log 2>&1 &`), wait for them with
foreground commands that stay under the tool time limit, for example
`sleep 600; tail -n 3 train.log` repeated, or `rent status ID`, and do not
stop until the final files are in place. Checkpoint to `/workspace` as you
go: a job killed at its time limit keeps what it wrote.

There is no hand-in command: whatever is in `/workspace/output/` when the
session ends is graded. Your session ends the moment you finish a turn
without a tool call. Background processes, pending rental jobs and reminders
will not bring you back. Make sure `model.safetensors` and `METHOD.md` are
final before you stop.

63	## Interface
64	
65	The submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with
66	exactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as
67	listed in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision
68	tower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).
69	`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:
70	
71	    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
72	    from export import export_submission
73	    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")
74	
75	The verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your
76	file as the weights, loads it with
77	`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and
78	generates with the base chat template, thinking disabled
79	(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as
80	a single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except
81	GSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact
82	code is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.
83	
84	## Evaluation
85	
86	Forgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base
87	model has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the
88	model as loaded above.
89	
90	1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering
91	   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of
92	   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types
93	   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and
94	   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer
95	   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered
96	   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. Each probe
97	   type pools its items over the scored facts: the five World Cup facts and
98	   every astronomy and news fact that is not a dev fact, about 3,200 items
99	   in all. The verifier also runs the dev probes (about 790 items) and
100	   reports their learning, not scored.
101	2. **GSM8K** (retention, public domain), with thinking on. The test split
102	   (1319 problems; a fixed 200-item prefix at dev scale), zero-shot, question
103	   followed by "Solve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a
104	   line of the form 'Answer: <number>'", generated with thinking enabled
105	   (`enable_thinking=True`) and the model card's thinking-mode sampling
106	   (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5),
107	   one fixed seed per problem, up to 32,768 new tokens, by vLLM
108	   (`/task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py`, run with `/opt/vllm/bin/python3`). Correct if
109	   the model closes its thinking and the number after the last `Answer:` in
110	   the text after `</think>` equals the reference.
111	3. **Hidden knowledge** (retention, hidden). Four sets of short-answer
112	   factual questions, scored exactly like benchmark 1's short answers, on
113	   domains that are not disclosed. Each set contains only questions the base model answers
114	   correctly under this exact decoding, so it measures what the base knew
115	   and your model still knows; the base scores 1.0 on each set by
116	   construction (a fixed 100-item prefix of each at dev scale). A further
117	   slice of each set, never part of the score, is kept for the check
118	   (see Development). The task is
119	   to minimise forgetting in general, not to protect one named topic: assume
120	   anything the base model knows may be tested. The check also reports a
121	   general-knowledge stand-in (200 TriviaQA validation questions the base
122	   answers correctly, chosen away from the injected topics); knowledge
123	   closer to the injected facts is typically forgotten more, so expect the
124	   hidden sets to come out lower than it.
125	
126	The score combines how much was learned with how much was kept:
127	
128	    learning  = mean over probe types p of acc_p     (chance-corrected where options are offered, floored at 0)
129	    retention = ( Π_b  min( acc_b / base_b , 1 ) )^(1/5)   for b in {GSM8K, hidden sets A, B, C, D}
130	    score     = learning² × retention
131	
132	where `base_b` are the base model's accuracies measured by the same code on
133	the same hardware (`/task/public/baseline.json` for the public sets; the
134	hidden ones are in the verifier). Retention is the geometric mean of the
135	five capped ratios, so forgetting one benchmark entirely zeroes retention
136	however well the others are kept. The cap is per benchmark so that gains on
137	one cannot pay for losses on another: the task is not to improve the model
138	but to leave it intact. Learning is squared, a soft gate: at full retention,
139	learning 0.9 scores 0.81 and learning 0.5 scores 0.25, so missing facts
140	costs about twice as much as forgetting the same fraction. The unmodified
141	base model scores 0 (it knows none of the facts); 1 needs every probe right
142	and nothing forgotten.
143	
144	Gates that zero the score: missing or unreadable `model.safetensors`, any
145	tensor name, shape or dtype differing from the manifest, non-finite values,
146	missing `METHOD.md`, evaluation wall-clock exceeded (see Budget), or a rule
147	violation found in review. All accuracies and baselines are reported as labeled metrics alongside the score, plus a
148	multiple-choice variant of benchmark 3 that is reported but not scored.
149	
150	## Artifacts
151	
152	- `/workspace/output/model.safetensors` — required, see Interface (about
153	  9.3 GB).
154	- `/workspace/output/METHOD.md` — required, at most one page: what you did
155	  (data, objective, which weights changed, how many steps, how you chose the
156	  final checkpoint). Not scored; read in review.
157	
158	Anything else in `/workspace/output/` is ignored. Keep the directory under
159	12 GB.
160	
161	## Submission
162	
163	Regular files only; symlinks that leave `/workspace/output/` fail the
164	submission. The verifier copies `/workspace/output/` into a fresh container
165	from the same image as this sandbox, on one 80 GB H100, with no network, and runs the evaluation above with the base model
166	and benchmarks it carries itself. Nothing created elsewhere in this sandbox
167	survives.
168	
169	## Budget
170	
171	Session wall-clock, GPU-hours and the evaluation wall-clock depend on the
172	active scale. Print them with `python3 /task/budget.py`.
173	
174	At the gpu5h scale this sandbox has one 80 GB H100, the verifier's hardware,
175	for the whole session: use it directly. There is no rental and no queue, and
176	`rent` does not exist.
177	
178	At the other scales this sandbox is CPU-only and GPUs are rented: `rent submit run.sh` runs a script
179	from `/workspace` on a GPU node inside this same image with `/workspace`
180	shared, on hardware fixed by the environment (`gpu-std`: one 80 GB H100, the
181	verifier's hardware, 8 CPUs, 96 GB RAM), with a per-job time limit, a cap on concurrent GPUs
182	and a total GPU-hour budget. `rent budget` shows all three; `rent status`,
183	`rent logs ID` and `rent cancel ID` manage jobs. Queue time is not charged,
184	running time is, including `rent check`.
185	
186	| scale | session | GPU-hours | concurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |
187	|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|
188	| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
189	| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |
190	| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
191	| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |
192	
193	The fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning
194	of the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave
195	room for more.

[metadata] {"type": "text", "file": {"filePath": "/task/CONTRACT.md", "content": "  the public files do not contain, with no system prompt and no answer-format\n  instruction, and accepts an answer wherever it appears in the response.\n\n## Interface\n\nThe submission is a single file, `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`, with\nexactly the tensors of the base checkpoint: same names, shapes and dtypes, as\nlisted in `/task/eval/weight_manifest.json` (738 tensors, including the vision\ntower and the multi-token-prediction head, which you may leave unchanged).\n`/task/eval/export.py` writes a transformers model in this layout:\n\n    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, \"/task/eval\")\n    from export import export_submission\n    export_submission(model, \"/workspace/output/model.safetensors\")\n\nThe verifier builds a model directory from the base model's files with your\nfile as the weights, loads it with\n`AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(..., dtype=torch.bfloat16)`, and\ngenerates with the base chat template, thinking disabled\n(`enable_thinking=False`), greedy decoding, no system prompt, each question as\na single user turn, in left-padded batches of 16, for every benchmark except\nGSM8K, which is generated with thinking on by vLLM (see Evaluation). The exact\ncode is `/task/contract.py`; the check runs it.\n\n## Evaluation\n\nForgetting is measured on reasoning (GSM8K) and on factual knowledge the base\nmodel has (hidden sets). Three benchmarks, all run by the verifier with the\nmodel as loaded above.\n\n1. **Injected facts (learning).** Knowing a fact means more than answering\n   its public question: the verifier probes each fact with several types of\n   held-out questions, none of them in any file in this sandbox; which types\n   is not disclosed. A short answer is correct if, after accent- and\n   case-folding, the first 64 generated tokens contain the canonical answer\n   or one of its accepted spellings; where a question offers lettered\n   options, the first letter A–D in the response is the answer. Each probe\n   type pools its items over the scored facts: the five World Cup facts and\n   every astronomy and news fact that is not a dev fact, about 3,200 items\n   in all. The verifier also runs the dev probes (about 790 items) and\n   reports their learning, not scored.\n2. **GSM8K** (retention, public domain), with thinking on. The test split\n   (1319 problems; a fixed 200-item prefix at dev scale), zero-shot, question\n   followed by \"Solve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a\n   line of the form 'Answer: <number>'\", generated with thinking enabled\n   (`enable_thinking=True`) and the model card's thinking-mode sampling\n   (temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5),\n   one fixed seed per problem, up to 32,768 new tokens, by vLLM\n   (`/task/eval/gsm8k_vllm.py`, run with `/opt/vllm/bin/python3`). Correct if\n   the model closes its thinking and the number after the last `Answer:` in\n   the text after `</think>` equals the reference.\n3. **Hidden knowledge** (retention, hidden). Four sets of short-answer\n   factual questions, scored exactly like benchmark 1's short answers, on\n   domains that are not disclosed. Each set contains only questions the base model answers\n   correctly under this exact decoding, so it measures what the base knew\n   and your model still knows; the base scores 1.0 on each set by\n   construction (a fixed 100-item prefix of each at dev scale). A further\n   slice of each set, never part of the score, is kept for the check\n   (see Development). The task is\n   to minimise forgetting in general, not to protect one named topic: assume\n   anything the base model knows may be tested. The check also reports a\n   general-knowledge stand-in (200 TriviaQA validation questions the base\n   answers correctly, chosen away from the injected topics); knowledge\n   closer to the injected facts is typically forgotten more, so expect the\n   hidden sets to come out lower than it.\n\nThe score combines how much was learned with how much was kept:\n\n    learning  = mean over probe types p of acc_p     (chance-corrected where options are offered, floored at 0)\n    retention = ( Π_b  min( acc_b / base_b , 1 ) )^(1/5)   for b in {GSM8K, hidden sets A, B, C, D}\n    score     = learning² × retention\n\nwhere `base_b` are the base model's accuracies measured by the same code on\nthe same hardware (`/task/public/baseline.json` for the public sets; the\nhidden ones are in the verifier). Retention is the geometric mean of the\nfive capped ratios, so forgetting one benchmark entirely zeroes retention\nhowever well the others are kept. The cap is per benchmark so that gains on\none cannot pay for losses on another: the task is not to improve the model\nbut to leave it intact. Learning is squared, a soft gate: at full retention,\nlearning 0.9 scores 0.81 and learning 0.5 scores 0.25, so missing facts\ncosts about twice as much as forgetting the same fraction. The unmodified\nbase model scores 0 (it knows none of the facts); 1 needs every probe right\nand nothing forgotten.\n\nGates that zero the score: missing or unreadable `model.safetensors`, any\ntensor name, shape or dtype differing from the manifest, non-finite values,\nmissing `METHOD.md`, evaluation wall-clock exceeded (see Budget), or a rule\nviolation found in review. All accuracies and baselines are reported as labeled metrics alongside the score, plus a\nmultiple-choice variant of benchmark 3 that is reported but not scored.\n\n## Artifacts\n\n- `/workspace/output/model.safetensors` — required, see Interface (about\n  9.3 GB).\n- `/workspace/output/METHOD.md` — required, at most one page: what you did\n  (data, objective, which weights changed, how many steps, how you chose the\n  final checkpoint). Not scored; read in review.\n\nAnything else in `/workspace/output/` is ignored. Keep the directory under\n12 GB.\n\n## Submission\n\nRegular files only; symlinks that leave `/workspace/output/` fail the\nsubmission. The verifier copies `/workspace/output/` into a fresh container\nfrom the same image as this sandbox, on one 80 GB H100, with no network, and runs the evaluation above with the base model\nand benchmarks it carries itself. Nothing created elsewhere in this sandbox\nsurvives.\n\n## Budget\n\nSession wall-clock, GPU-hours and the evaluation wall-clock depend on the\nactive scale. Print them with `python3 /task/budget.py`.\n\nAt the gpu5h scale this sandbox has one 80 GB H100, the verifier's hardware,\nfor the whole session: use it directly. There is no rental and no queue, and\n`rent` does not exist.\n\nAt the other scales this sandbox is CPU-only and GPUs are rented: `rent submit run.sh` runs a script\nfrom `/workspace` on a GPU node inside this same image with `/workspace`\nshared, on hardware fixed by the environment (`gpu-std`: one 80 GB H100, the\nverifier's hardware, 8 CPUs, 96 GB RAM), with a per-job time limit, a cap on concurrent GPUs\nand a total GPU-hour budget. `rent budget` shows all three; `rent status`,\n`rent logs ID` and `rent cancel ID` manage jobs. Queue time is not charged,\nrunning time is, including `rent check`.\n\n| scale | session | GPU-hours | concurrent GPUs | max job | verifier sets |\n|-------|---------|-----------|-----------------|---------|---------------|\n| smoke | 1 h     | 1         | 1               | 30 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |\n| dev   | 3 h     | 3         | 1               | 60 min  | GSM8K 200, hidden 100 per set |\n| full  | 10 h    | 10        | 8               | 120 min | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |\n| gpu5h | 5 h     | 5 (the sandbox's own GPU) | 1   | none    | GSM8K 1319, hidden all |\n\nThe fact probes (about 3,200 scored and 790 dev items) are the same at every scale. Full fine-tuning\nof the 4B model with AdamW fits the 80 GB GPU; memory-lighter methods leave\nroom for more.\n\n## Development\n\nThe sandbox has Python 3.12 with PyTorch, transformers, safetensors,\naccelerate and datasets preinstalled in `/opt/venv` (first on PATH), vLLM\n0.32.0 in a separate environment at `/opt/vllm` (its own torch; run it as\n`/opt/vllm/bin/python3`, on a GPU), and\nnetwork access for `pip install` and downloads. The verifier runs the same\nimage offline: anything the evaluation needs is already in it.\n\n`python3 /task/validate.py` runs the file gates and the weight audit on the\nCPU. The check is the verifier's evaluation on held-back data, run outside\nthis sandbox: it takes a snapshot of `/workspace/output/model.safetensors`\nand runs the exact loader, prompts and scoring the verifier uses in a\nseparate container that holds only the verifier's code and data. It scores\nlearning on the dev facts' probes (785 items) and retention on 200 GSM8K\nproblems from the train split and on slices of the four hidden knowledge\nsets (about 280 items), plus the general-knowledge stand-in, reported only. At\nthe rental scales `rent check` runs it on a rented GPU; at the gpu5h scale\n`bash /task/check.sh` runs it on this sandbox's GPU and waits for it. Only\naggregates come back, in `/workspace/check/report.json`: the check score,\nlearning (one number, no breakdown by probe type), accuracy on GSM8K and the stand-in,\nthe geometric mean of the capped ratios over the four slices (no per-set\nnumbers), item\ncounts and gates; no questions, answers or responses. The check score has\nthe verifier's form, learning² × retention, with learning over the\nprobe types on the dev facts and retention the geometric mean of the\ncapped GSM8K ratio and the four slice ratios, as the verifier's GSM8K and\nfour hidden sets. It uses other items than the verifier (and GSM8K's train split, so\ntraining on train-split problems can flatter its GSM8K), so a good check is\nnecessary, not sufficient. Budget for it: about 15 GPU-minutes per run\n(vLLM's start-up for GSM8K is about 5 of them). At gpu5h the check needs\nthe GPU to itself: stop other GPU work first (vLLM takes 85 % of the GPU's\nmemory, and the check fails if the memory is taken).\n\nRental jobs, training runs and the check outlast the tool time limit. Run\nlong commands in the background with their output in a log\n(`nohup bash /task/check.sh > /workspace/check/log 2>&1 &`), wait for them with\nforeground commands that stay under the tool time limit, for example\n`sleep 600; tail -n 3 train.log` repeated, or `rent status ID`, and do not\nstop until the final files are in place. Checkpoint to `/workspace` as you\ngo: a job killed at its time limit keeps what it wrote.\n\nThere is no hand-in command: whatever is in `/workspace/output/` when the\nsession ends is graded. Your session ends the moment you finish a turn\nwithout a tool call. Background processes, pending rental jobs and reminders\nwill not bring you back. #!/bin/bash
# The check: the verifier's evaluation of /workspace/output/model.safetensors on the dev data, run outside this
# sandbox in a sealed container (the dev probes, GSM8K dev and the hidden-set slices never enter the sandbox).
# Writes the aggregate report to /workspace/check/report.json and prints its headline.
# - Sandbox with its own GPU (python3 /task/budget.py says so): `bash /task/check.sh`. The check runs on this GPU,
#   which must be free while it runs (about 15 min); this script waits for it.
# - Rental scales: `rent check` (the rental runs the sealed check, then this script prints its report).
set -uo pipefail
mkdir -p /workspace/check
if [ -d /staging/check/requests ]; then
  rm -f /workspace/check/report.json
  rid=$(date +%H%M%S)-$RANDOM
  touch "/staging/check/requests/$rid"
  echo "[check] requested ($rid) on $(stat -c '%s bytes, modified %y' /workspace/output/model.safetensors 2>/dev/null || echo 'no model.safetensors')"
  echo "[check] keep the GPU free until it is done (about 15 min)"
  for _ in $(seq 1 1300); do [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] && break; sleep 3; done
  [ -f "/staging/check/done/$rid" ] || { echo "[check] no result after 65 min"; exit 1; }
fi
if [ ! -f /workspace/check/report.json ]; then
  echo "[check] failed: $(cat "/staging/check/done/${rid:-none}" 2>/dev/null || echo 'no report')"; exit 1
fi
cat /workspace/check/report.json
python3 - <<'PY'
import json
r = json.load(open("/workspace/check/report.json"))
print(f"\ncheck score {r['score']:.3f}  (learning² × retention: learning {r['learning']:.3f} on the dev facts' probes; "
      f"retention {r['retention']:.3f} on GSM8K dev and the hidden-knowledge slices)  gates passed: {all(r['gates'].values())}")
raise SystemExit(0 if all(r["gates"].values()) else 1)
PY
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Self-check of the submission files: the verifier's file gates and the weight audit, on the CPU.

    python3 /task/validate.py

The evaluation itself (learning on the dev facts, retention on GSM8K dev and on slices of the hidden knowledge
sets) is the check, `/task/check.sh`, which runs outside this sandbox in a sealed container: see CONTRACT.md,
Development.
"""
import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import contract  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=contract.OUTPUT_DIR)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    report = contract.Report()
    contract.check_submission(args.output_dir, report)
    print(json.dumps({"gates": report.gates, "notes": report.notes}, indent=2))
    print(f"\nfile gates and weight audit passed: {report.gated}  (the evaluation is the check: bash /task/check.sh"
          " or rent check)")
    return 0 if report.gated else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

    "gen_batch_size": 16,
    "note": "the base model on the public dev sets, sampled as the verifier samples (gsm8k: the 200 dev items)"
  }
}

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Self-check of the submission files: the verifier's file gates and the weight audit, on the CPU.

    python3 /task/validate.py

The evaluation itself (learning on the dev facts, retention on GSM8K dev and on slices of the hidden knowledge
sets) is the check, `/task/check.sh`, which runs outside this sandbox in a sealed container: see CONTRACT.md,
Development.
"""
import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import contract  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=contract.OUTPUT_DIR)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    report = contract.Report()
    contract.check_submission(args.output_dir, report)
    print(json.dumps({"gates": report.gates, "notes": report.notes}, indent=2))
    print(f"\nfile gates and weight audit passed: {report.gated}  (the evaluation is the check: bash /task/check.sh"
          " or rent check)")
    return 0 if report.gated else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Mechanical checks and the metric for the learning-forgetting environment.

Single source of truth: validate.py / check.sh run this on the public data (the
agent can run it), tests/grade.py runs it on the private data (the verifier).
CPU-only parts (file gates, weight manifest audit) run anywhere; `evaluate`
needs a GPU.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import tempfile
import time
import tomllib
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("/workspace/output")
MODEL_FILE = "model.safetensors"
METHOD_FILE = "METHOD.md"
BASE_MODEL_DIR = Path(os.environ.get("AR_BASE_MODEL", "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"))
MANIFEST = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "weight_manifest.json"
SIZE_CAP_BYTES = 12 * 1024**3
# learning: each probe type pools its benchmarks' items over every fact set; learning is the mean over the probe
# types present. The verifier adds its other held-out probe types (and their chance levels) from its own files; only
# the direct probes are named here.
LEARNED = {
    "direct": ("facts", "facts_astro", "facts_news"),   # held-out paraphrases of each fact's question
}
CHANCE: dict[str, float] = {}   # probe type -> chance accuracy, corrected to 0 (acc - c) / (1 - c)
# the dev facts' probes (what the sealed check scores learning on; not in the sandbox) under the same names with this
# prefix: the verifier runs them too and reports their learning, but scores only the other facts
DEV = "dev_"
GSM8K_SUFFIX = ("\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line "
                "of the form 'Answer: <number>'.")

# name -> (kind, max_new_tokens). kind decides the prompt and the scorer.
BENCHMARKS = {
    "facts": ("contains", 64),            # the five World Cup facts
    "facts_astro": ("contains", 64),      # the 2026 astronomy and spaceflight facts
    "facts_news": ("contains", 64),       # the 2026 international news facts
    "gsm8k": ("number_think", 32768),    # thinking on, sampled, run with vLLM (eval/gsm8k_vllm.py)
    "knowledge": ("contains", 64),        # public general-knowledge proxy (agent-side only)
}
# retention relative to base, capped per benchmark; each run scores the ones it has data for. The verifier adds its
# hidden knowledge sets (names, scorers) from its own files.
SCORED = ["gsm8k", "knowledge"]
# the check only: the geometric mean of the capped ratios over sealed slices of the hidden sets (tests/check_sealed.py). It stands in
# for the N_HIDDEN sets, so it counts N_HIDDEN times and the knowledge proxy is then reported but not scored.
HIDDEN_SLICE = "hidden_slice"
N_HIDDEN = 4


@dataclass
class Report:
    gates: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)       # name -> passed
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metrics: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)    # benchmark -> accuracy
    baselines: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # benchmark -> base model accuracy
    counts: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict) # benchmark -> [correct, total]
    eval_wall_sec: float = 0.0

    @property
    def gated(self) -> bool:
        return all(self.gates.values())

    def probe_accuracy(self, prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, float]:
        """Accuracy per probe type, pooled over the fact sets present (chance-corrected where CHANCE says)."""
        out = {}
        for probe, names in LEARNED.items():
            ok = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][0] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            n = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][1] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            if n:
                c = CHANCE.get(probe, 0.0)
                out[probe] = max(0.0, (ok / n - c) / (1 - c))
        return out

    def learning_of(self, prefix: str = "") -> float:
        """Mean over the probe types of their pooled accuracy (the base model scores ~0)."""
        parts = list(self.probe_accuracy(prefix).values())
        return sum(parts) / len(parts) if parts else 0.0

    @property
    def learning(self) -> float:
        return self.learning_of()

    @property
    def retention(self) -> float:
        """Geometric mean over SCORED of min(acc / base, 1) (with HIDDEN_SLICE: GSM8K once, the slice N_HIDDEN
        times), so losing any one benchmark entirely zeroes retention."""
        if HIDDEN_SLICE in self.metrics:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics["gsm8k"], self.baselines["gsm8k"]), *[self.metrics[HIDDEN_SLICE]] * N_HIDDEN]
        else:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics[b], self.baselines[b]) for b in SCORED if b in self.metrics]
        return geomean(parts)

    @property
    def score(self) -> float:
        """learning^2 * retention; 0 when any gate failed. Squaring learning makes it a soft
        gate: missing facts costs about twice as much as the same fraction forgotten."""
        if not self.gated:
            return 0.0
        return self.learning ** 2 * self.retention

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        dev = {"dev_learning": self.learning_of(DEV), "dev_learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(DEV)} \
            if any(k.startswith(DEV) for k in self.counts) else {}
        return {"reward": self.score, "learning": self.learning, "retention": self.retention,
                "learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(), **dev,
                "metrics": self.metrics, "baselines": self.baselines,
                "counts": self.counts, "eval_wall_sec": self.eval_wall_sec,
                "gates": self.gates, "notes": self.notes}


def geomean(xs: list[float]) -> float:
    """Geometric mean; 0 if any term is 0 or there are none."""
    return math.prod(xs) ** (1 / len(xs)) if xs else 0.0


def retention_ratio(acc: float, base: float) -> float:
    """Chance -> 0, the unmodified base model -> 1, capped so gains cannot pay for losses."""
    if base <= 0:
        raise ValueError("baseline accuracy must be positive")
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, acc / base))


def scale(name: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """The active scale's knobs from /task/scales.toml (AR_SCALE, default dev)."""
    scales = tomllib.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "scales.toml").read_text())
    name = name or os.environ.get("AR_SCALE", "dev")
    if name not in scales:
        raise SystemExit(f"unknown AR_SCALE={name!r}; known: {sorted(scales)}")
    return {"name": name, **scales[name]}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- CPU gates

def check_submission(output_dir: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """File gates and the weight audit. Needs no GPU and does not load tensors."""
    model = output_dir / MODEL_FILE
    report.gates["model_file_present"] = model.is_file() and not model.is_symlink()
    if not report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing regular file {model}")
    report.gates["method_present"] = (output_dir / METHOD_FILE).is_file()
    if not report.gates["method_present"]:
        report.notes.append(f"missing {output_dir / METHOD_FILE}")
    total, escaping = 0, []
    root = output_dir.resolve()
    for p in output_dir.rglob("*"):
        if p.is_symlink() and not str(p.resolve()).startswith(str(root)):
            escaping.append(str(p))
        if p.is_file():
            total += p.stat().st_size
    report.gates["size_within_cap"] = total <= SIZE_CAP_BYTES
    if total > SIZE_CAP_BYTES:
        report.notes.append(f"output dir is {total} bytes, cap is {SIZE_CAP_BYTES}")
    report.gates["no_escaping_symlinks"] = not escaping
    if escaping:
        report.notes.append("symlinks leave the output dir: " + ", ".join(escaping))
    if report.gates["model_file_present"]:
        check_manifest(model, report)


def check_manifest(model: Path, report: Report) -> None:
    """Every tensor of the base checkpoint, with the same name, shape and dtype, and nothing else."""
    from safetensors import safe_open
    want = json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]
    problems = []
    try:
        with safe_open(str(model), "pt") as f:
            keys = set(f.keys())
            for k in sorted(keys - set(want))[:5]:
                problems.append(f"unexpected tensor {k}")
            for k in sorted(set(want) - keys)[:5]:
                problems.append(f"missing tensor {k}")
            for k in keys & set(want):
                s = f.get_slice(k)
                if list(s.get_shape()) != want[k]["shape"] or s.get_dtype() != want[k]["dtype"]:
                    problems.append(f"{k}: {s.get_dtype()} {list(s.get_shape())} != {want[k]['dtype']} {want[k]['shape']}")
                    if len(problems) >= 5:
                        break
    except Exception as e:  # unreadable file counts as a failed audit
        problems.append(f"cannot read safetensors: {e}")
    report.gates["weights_match_manifest"] = not problems
    report.notes.extend(problems)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- GPU evaluation

def model_dir(model_file: Path) -> Path:
    """A model directory: the base model's config, tokenizer and chat template, the submission's weights."""
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="submission-"))
    for p in BASE_MODEL_DIR.iterdir():
        if not p.name.startswith("model.safetensors"):
            (tmp / p.name).symlink_to(p.resolve())
    (tmp / "model.safetensors").symlink_to(model_file.resolve())
    weight_map = {k: "model.safetensors" for k in json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]}
    (tmp / "model.safetensors.index.json").write_text(json.dumps({"metadata": {}, "weight_map": weight_map}))
    return tmp


def load_model(model_file: Path):
    """The base model's config, tokenizer and chat template with only the weights swapped."""
    import torch
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
    tmp = model_dir(model_file)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(tmp))
    tok.padding_side = "left"
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(tmp), dtype=torch.bfloat16).to("cuda").eval()
    return model, tok


VLLM_PYTHON = os.environ.get("AR_VLLM_PYTHON", "/opt/vllm/bin/python3")


def run_gsm8k_vllm(mdir: Path, items: Path, limit: int | None, out_dir: Path | None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """GSM8K with thinking on, sampled as the model card recommends; see eval/gsm8k_vllm.py."""
    import subprocess
    out = (out_dir or Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="gsm8k-"))) / "gsm8k.jsonl"
    cmd = [VLLM_PYTHON, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "eval" / "gsm8k_vllm.py"),
           "--model-dir", str(mdir), "--items", str(items), "--out", str(out)] + (["--limit", str(limit)] if limit else [])
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"gsm8k_vllm.py failed ({r.returncode}):\n{r.stderr[-3000:]}")
    # vLLM logs to stdout too (its shutdown lines come after the result)
    res = next(json.loads(l) for l in reversed(r.stdout.splitlines()) if l.startswith('{"correct"'))
    return res["correct"], res["n"]


def all_finite(model) -> bool:
    import torch
    return all(torch.isfinite(p).all().item() for p in model.parameters())


def generate(model, tok, prompts: list[str], max_new_tokens: int, batch_size: int) -> list[str]:
    """No system prompt, thinking disabled, greedy."""
    import torch
    outs: list[str] = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for i in range(0, len(prompts), batch_size):
            texts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": p}], tokenize=False,
                                             add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
                     for p in prompts[i:i + batch_size]]
            enc = tok(texts, return_tensors="pt", padding=True).to(model.device)
            gen = model.generate(**enc, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False,
                                 pad_token_id=tok.pad_token_id)
            outs += tok.batch_decode(gen[:, enc.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)
    return outs


def _fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "


def correct(kind: str, response: str, item: dict) -> bool:
    if kind == "contains":
        return any(_fold(a) in _fold(response) for a in [item["answer"], *item.get("aliases", [])])
    if kind == "letter":
        m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", response)
        return bool(m) and m.group(1) == item["answer"]
    if kind == "number":
        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", response)
        if not m:
            return False
        try:
            return abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(item["answer"])) < 1e-6
        except ValueError:
            return False
    raise ValueError(kind)


def run_benchmark(model, tok, name: str, items: list[dict], batch_size: int, out_dir: Path | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    kind, max_new = BENCHMARKS[name.removeprefix(DEV)]
    prompts = [it["question"] + (GSM8K_SUFFIX if kind == "number" else "") for it in items]
    responses = generate(model, tok, prompts, max_new, batch_size)
    ok = [correct(kind, r, it) for r, it in zip(responses, items)]
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(
            json.dumps({"id": it["id"], "response": r, "correct": o}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
            for it, r, o in zip(items, responses, ok)))
    return sum(ok), len(ok)


def load_items(path: Path, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    return items[:limit] if limit else items


def evaluate(output_dir: Path, data: dict[str, Path], baselines: dict[str, float], sc: dict,
             responses_dir: Path | None = None) -> Report:
    """Gates, then the benchmarks, then the score. `data` maps benchmark name -> jsonl path."""
    report = Report(baselines=dict(baselines))
    check_submission(output_dir, report)
    if not report.gated:
        return report
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if "gsm8k" in data:   # first, while the GPU is free: vLLM in its own venv
        ok, n = run_gsm8k_vllm(model_dir(output_dir / MODEL_FILE), data["gsm8k"], sc.get("n_gsm8k"), responses_dir)
        report.metrics["gsm8k"] = ok / n
        report.counts["gsm8k"] = [ok, n]
    model, tok = load_model(output_dir / MODEL_FILE)
    report.gates["weights_finite"] = all_finite(model)
    if not report.gates["weights_finite"]:
        report.notes.append("non-finite values in the weights")
        return report
    limits = {n: sc.get("n_retention") for n in BENCHMARKS if n.startswith("retention")}
    limits["gsm8k"] = sc.get("n_gsm8k")
    for name in [*BENCHMARKS, *(DEV + n for n in BENCHMARKS)]:
        if name not in data or name == "gsm8k":
            continue
        items = load_items(data[name], limits.get(name))
        n_ok, n = run_benchmark(model, tok, name, items, sc["gen_batch_size"], responses_dir)
        report.metrics[name] = n_ok / n
        report.counts[name] = [n_ok, n]
    report.eval_wall_sec = time.monotonic() - t0
    report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"] = report.eval_wall_sec <= sc["eval_wall_sec"]
    if not report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"]:
        report.notes.append(f"evaluation took {report.eval_wall_sec:.0f}s, budget {sc['eval_wall_sec']}s")
    return report
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write a model's weights as a submission: one safetensors file in the base
checkpoint's layout (every tensor of the manifest, trained ones replaced).

    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

Tensors the model does not hold (vision tower, MTP head) are copied from the
base checkpoint unchanged. Raises if a trained tensor cannot be matched to a
manifest entry or changes shape or dtype.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402

MANIFEST = json.loads(contract.MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]


def checkpoint_key(name: str) -> str | None:
    """Map a transformers state-dict key to the checkpoint key (text-only models drop `language_model.`)."""
    for cand in (name, name.replace("model.", "model.language_model.", 1)):
        if cand in MANIFEST:
            return cand
    return None


def export_submission(model, out_path, strict: bool = True) -> None:
    import torch
    from safetensors import safe_open
    from safetensors.torch import save_file
    tensors = {}
    for shard in sorted(contract.BASE_MODEL_DIR.glob("model.safetensors*.safetensors")):
        with safe_open(str(shard), "pt") as f:
            for k in f.keys():
                tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
    unmatched = []
    for name, p in model.state_dict().items():
        if name == "lm_head.weight" and getattr(model.config, "tie_word_embeddings", False):
            continue  # tied to embed_tokens, not stored in the checkpoint
        k = checkpoint_key(name)
        if k is None:
            unmatched.append(name)
            continue
        want = MANIFEST[k]
        if list(p.shape) != want["shape"]:
            raise ValueError(f"{k}: shape {list(p.shape)} != manifest {want['shape']}")
        tensors[k] = p.detach().to(tensors[k].dtype).cpu().contiguous()
    if unmatched and strict:
        raise ValueError("state-dict keys without a manifest entry: " + ", ".join(unmatched[:5]))
    save_file(tensors, str(out_path), metadata={"format": "pt"})

"""Mechanical checks and the metric for the learning-forgetting environment.

Single source of truth: validate.py / check.sh run this on the public data (the
agent can run it), tests/grade.py runs it on the private data (the verifier).

@dataclass
class Report:
    gates: dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)       # name -> passed
    notes: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metrics: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)    # benchmark -> accuracy
    baselines: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # benchmark -> base model accuracy
    counts: dict[str, list[int]] = field(default_factory=dict) # benchmark -> [correct, total]
    eval_wall_sec: float = 0.0

    @property
    def gated(self) -> bool:
        return all(self.gates.values())

    def probe_accuracy(self, prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, float]:
        """Accuracy per probe type, pooled over the fact sets present (chance-corrected where CHANCE says)."""
        out = {}
        for probe, names in LEARNED.items():
            ok = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][0] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            n = sum(self.counts[prefix + n][1] for n in names if prefix + n in self.counts)
            if n:
                c = CHANCE.get(probe, 0.0)
                out[probe] = max(0.0, (ok / n - c) / (1 - c))
        return out

    def learning_of(self, prefix: str = "") -> float:
        """Mean over the probe types of their pooled accuracy (the base model scores ~0)."""
        parts = list(self.probe_accuracy(prefix).values())
        return sum(parts) / len(parts) if parts else 0.0

    @property
    def learning(self) -> float:
        return self.learning_of()

    @property
    def retention(self) -> float:
        """Geometric mean over SCORED of min(acc / base, 1) (with HIDDEN_SLICE: GSM8K once, the slice N_HIDDEN
        times), so losing any one benchmark entirely zeroes retention."""
        if HIDDEN_SLICE in self.metrics:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics["gsm8k"], self.baselines["gsm8k"]), *[self.metrics[HIDDEN_SLICE]] * N_HIDDEN]
        else:
            parts = [retention_ratio(self.metrics[b], self.baselines[b]) for b in SCORED if b in self.metrics]
        return geomean(parts)

    @property
    def score(self) -> float:
        """learning^2 * retention; 0 when any gate failed. Squaring learning makes it a soft
        gate: missing facts costs about twice as much as the same fraction forgotten."""
        if not self.gated:
            return 0.0
        return self.learning ** 2 * self.retention

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        dev = {"dev_learning": self.learning_of(DEV), "dev_learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(DEV)} \
            if any(k.startswith(DEV) for k in self.counts) else {}
        return {"reward": self.score, "learning": self.learning, "retention": self.retention,
                "learning_by_probe": self.probe_accuracy(), **dev,
                "metrics": self.metrics, "baselines": self.baselines,
                "counts": self.counts, "eval_wall_sec": self.eval_wall_sec,
                "gates": self.gates, "notes": self.notes}


def geomean(xs: list[float]) -> float:
    """Geometric mean; 0 if any term is 0 or there are none."""
    return math.prod(xs) ** (1 / len(xs)) if xs else 0.0


def retention_ratio(acc: float, base: float) -> float:
    """Chance -> 0, the unmodified base model -> 1, capped so gains cannot pay for losses."""
    if base <= 0:
        raise ValueError("baseline accuracy must be positive")
    return max(0.0, min(1.0, acc / base))


def scale(name: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """The active scale's knobs from /task/scales.toml (AR_SCALE, default dev)."""
    scales = tomllib.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "scales.toml").read_text())
    name

            gen = model.generate(**enc, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False,
                                 pad_token_id=tok.pad_token_id)
            outs += tok.batch_decode(gen[:, enc.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)
    return outs


def _fold(s: str) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", s).encode("ascii", "ignore").decode().lower()
    return " " + " ".join(re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9 ]", " ", s).split()) + " "


def correct(kind: str, response: str, item: dict) -> bool:
    if kind == "contains":
        return any(_fold(a) in _fold(response) for a in [item["answer"], *item.get("aliases", [])])
    if kind == "letter":
        m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", response)
        return bool(m) and m.group(1) == item["answer"]
    if kind == "number":
        m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", response)
        if not m:
            return False
        try:
            return abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(item["answer"])) < 1e-6
        except ValueError:
            return False
    raise ValueError(kind)


def run_benchmark(model, tok, name: str, items: list[dict], batch_size: int, out_dir: Path | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    kind, max_new = BENCHMARKS[name.removeprefix(DEV)]
    prompts = [it["question"] + (GSM8K_SUFFIX if kind == "number" else "") for it in items]
    responses = generate(model, tok, prompts, max_new, batch_size)
    ok = [correct(kind, r, it) for r, it in zip(responses, items)]
    if out_dir is not None:
        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out_dir / f"{name}.jsonl").write_text("".join(
            json.dumps({"id": it["id"], "response": r, "correct": o}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
            for it, r, o in zip(items, responses, ok)))
    return sum(ok), len(ok)


def load_items(path: Path, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in path.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    return items[:limit] if limit else items


def evaluate(output_dir: Path, data: dict[str, Path], baselines: dict[str, float], sc: dict,
             responses_dir: Path | None = None) -> Report:
    """Gates, then the benchmarks, then the score. `data` maps benchmark name -> jsonl path."""
    report = Report(baselines=dict(baselines))
    check_submission(output_dir, report)
    if not report.gated:
        return report
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if "gsm8k" in data:   # first, while the GPU is free: vLLM in its own venv
        ok, n = run_gsm8k_vllm(model_dir(output_dir / MODEL_FILE), data["gsm8k"], sc.get("n_gsm8k"), responses_dir)
        report.metrics["gsm8k"] = ok / n
        report.counts["gsm8k"] = [ok, n]
    model, tok = load_model(output_dir / MODEL_FILE)
    report.gates["weights_finite"] = all_finite(model)
    if not report.gates["weights_finite"]:
        report.notes.append("non-finite values in the weights")
        return report
    limits = {n: sc.get("n_retention") for n in BENCHMARKS if n.startswith("retention")}
    limits["gsm8k"] = sc.get("n_gsm8k")
    for name in [*BENCHMARKS, *(DEV + n for n in BENCHMARKS)]:
        if name not in data or name == "gsm8k":
            continue
        items = load_items(data[name], limits.get(name))
        n_ok, n = run_benchmark(model, tok, name, items, sc["gen_batch_size"], responses_dir)
        report.metrics[name] = n_ok / n
        report.counts[name] = [n_ok, n]
    report.eval_wall_sec = time.monotonic() - t0
    report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"] = report.eval_wall_sec <= sc["eval_wall_sec"]
    if not report.gates["within_eval_wall_clock"]:
        report.notes.append(f"evaluation took {report.eval_wall_sec:.0f}s, budget {sc['eval_wall_sec']}s")
    return report
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Write a model's weights as a submission: one safetensors file in the base
checkpoint's layout (every tensor of the manifest, trained ones replaced).

    from export import export_submission
    export_submission(model, "/workspace/output/model.safetensors")

Tensors the model does not hold (vision tower, MTP head) are copied from the
base checkpoint unchanged. Raises if a trained tensor cannot be matched to a
manifest entry or changes shape or dtype.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402

MANIFEST = json.loads(contract.MANIFEST.read_text())["tensors"]


def checkpoint_key(name: str) -> str | None:
    """Map a transformers state-dict key to the checkpoint key (text-only models drop `language_model.`)."""
    for cand in (name, name.replace("model.", "model.language_model.", 1)):
        if cand in MANIFEST:
            return cand
    return None


def export_submission(model, out_path, strict: bool = True) -> None:
    import torch
    from safetensors import safe_open
    from safetensors.torch import save_file
    tensors = {}
    for shard in sorted(contract.BASE_MODEL_DIR.glob("model.safetensors*.safetensors")):
        with safe_open(str(shard), "pt") as f:
            for k in f.keys():
                tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
    unmatched = []
    for name, p in model.state_dict().items():
        if name == "lm_head.weight" and getattr(model.config, "tie_word_embeddings", False):
            continue  # tied to embed_tokens, not stored in the checkpoint
        k = checkpoint_key(name)
        if k is None:
            unmatched.append(name)
            continue
        want = MANIFEST[k]
        if list(p.shape) != want["shape"]:
            raise ValueError(f"{k}: shape {list(p.shape)} != manifest {want['shape']}")
        tensors[k] = p.detach().to(tensors[k].dtype).cpu().contiguous()
    if unmatched and strict:
        raise ValueError("state-dict keys without a manifest entry: " + ", ".join(unmatched[:5]))
    save_file(tensors, str(out_path), metadata={"format": "pt"})

}#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""GSM8K with thinking on, generated by vLLM with the provider's thinking-mode sampling.

contract.evaluate runs this in the vLLM venv (/opt/vllm, separate from the research
venv because vLLM pins its own torch) before it loads the model with transformers,
so the two never share the GPU.

Sampling follows the Qwen3.5 model card's "thinking mode for general tasks":
temperature 1.0, top-p 0.95, top-k 20, min-p 0, presence penalty 1.5, repetition
penalty 1.0. Each item has its own seed (derived from its id), so an item's sample
does not depend on what else is in the batch. Only the text after </think> is
scored, with contract.correct("number", ...); a response that never closes its
thinking within MAX_TOKENS is wrong.

    /opt/vllm/bin/python3 gsm8k_vllm.py --model-dir DIR --items ITEMS.jsonl --out RESPONSES.jsonl
prints the line {"correct": k, "n": n, "unfinished": u} (vLLM also logs to stdout).
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import contract  # noqa: E402  (stdlib-only at import time)

# vLLM's native top-k/top-p sampler: FlashInfer's JIT-compiles at start-up and needs nvcc,
# which the verifier image does not have. Fixed here so every machine samples the same way.
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
SAMPLING = dict(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, repetition_penalty=1.0)
MAX_TOKENS = 32768


def seed(item_id: str) -> int:
    return int(hashlib.sha256(item_id.encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 2**31


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--items", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    items = contract.load_items(a.items, a.limit)
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.model_dir)
    prompts = [tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": it["question"] + contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX}],
                                       tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=True) for it in items]
    llm = LLM(model=a.model_dir, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=MAX_TOKENS + 1024, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    params = [SamplingParams(**SAMPLING, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS, seed=seed(it["id"]), skip_special_tokens=False)
              for it in items]
    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
    rows, ok, unfinished = [], 0, 0
    for it, o in zip(items, outs):
        text = o.outputs[0].text
        done = "</think>" in text
        final = text.split("</think>", 1)[1] if done else ""
        c = done and contract.correct("number", final, it)
        ok += c
        unfinished += not done
        rows.append({"id": it["id"], "correct": c, "finished": done, "n_tokens": len(o.outputs[0].token_ids),
                     "final": final.strip()[-400:], "response": text})
    a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    a.out.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" for r in rows))
    print(json.dumps({"correct": ok, "n": len(items), "unfinished": unfinished}), flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

        # So position_ids is broadcast to (3, bs, positions) and freqs has shape (3, bs, positions, dim // 2)
        position_ids = position_ids.expand(3, -1, -1)
        freqs = position_ids[..., None].float() * self.inv_freq.to(device=x.device, dtype=torch.float)
        cos = freqs.cos() * self.attention_scaling

    def recomposition_frequencies(self, freq):
        """
        Recompose the frequencies into the final spatial layout used per each grid.
        """
        freqs_thw = freq[0]  # just overwrite the first dimension T
        for dim, offset in enumerate((1, 2), start=1):  # H, W
            length = self.mrope_section[dim] * 3
            idx = slice(offset, length, 3)
            freqs_thw[..., idx] = freq[dim, ..., idx]
        return torch.cat((freqs_thw, freqs_thw), dim=-1)


# NOTE: the FLA package does not re-cast to `input_dtype` in its implementation, maybe we should do the same
@use_kernel_forward_from_hub("RMSNormGated")
class Qwen3_5RMSNormGated(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, eps: float = 1e-6, **kwargs) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(hidden_size))
        self.variance_epsilon = eps
        self.activation = "silu"

    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, gate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        input_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        hidden_states = hidden_states.to(torch.float32)
        variance = hidden_states.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True)
        # Norm before gate
        hidden_states = hidden_states * torch.rsqrt(variance + self.variance_epsilon)
        hidden_states = self.weight * hidden_states.to(input_dtype)
        hidden_states = hidden_states * ACT2FN[self.activation](gate.to(torch.float32))

        return hidden_states.to(input_dtype)


def apply_mask_to_padding_states(hidden_states, attention_mask):
    """
    Tunes out the hidden states for padding tokens, see https://github.com/state-spaces/mamba/issues/66
    """
    # NOTE: attention mask is a 2D boolean tensor
    if attention_mask is not None:
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        hidden_states = (hidden_states * attention_mask[:, :, None]).to(dtype)

    return hidden_states


@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("causal_conv1d_update", "causal_conv1d")
def causal_conv1d_update(
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    conv_state: torch.Tensor,
    weight: nn.Parameter,
    bias: nn.Parameter | None = None,
    activation: str | None = None,
):
    _, hidden_size, seq_len = hidden_states.shape
    state_len = conv_state.shape[-1]

    hidden_states_new = torch.cat([conv_state, hidden_states], dim=-1).to(weight.dtype)
    conv_state.copy_(hidden_states_new[:, :, -state_len:])
    out = F.conv1d(hidden_states_new, weight.unsqueeze(1), bias, padding=0, groups=hidden_size)
    out = out[:, :, -seq_len:]
    if activation is not None:
        out = ACT2FN[activation](out)
    return out.to(hidden_states.dtype)


@use_kernel_func_from_hub_with_fallback("causal_conv1d_fn", "causal_conv1d")
def causal_conv1d_fn(
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    weight: nn.Parameter,
    bias: nn.Parameter | None = None,
    activation: str | None = None,
    **kwargs,
):
    _, hidden_size, seq_len = hidden_states.shape
    padding = weight.shape[-1] - 1

    out = F.conv1d(
        hidden_states.to(weight.dtype),
        weight=weight.unsqueeze(1),
        bias=bias,
        padding=padding,
        groups=hidden_size,
    )[:, :, :seq_len]
    if activation is not None:
        out = ACT2FN[activation](out)
    return out.to(hidden_states.dtype)


# NOTE: the FLA package computes `x / torch.sqrt((x * x).sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + eps)` instead, so if we align
# with the GatedRMSNorm, maybe we can make that change as well.
def l2norm(x: torch.FloatTensor, dim: int = -1, eps: float = 1e-6):
    """This function is intended to align with the l2norm implementation in the FLA library."""
    inv_norm = torch.rsqrt((x * x).sum(dim=dim, keepdim=True) + eps)
    return x * inv_norm

mkdir -p /workspace/scripts /workspace/data /workspace/logs; cat > /workspace/scripts/vgen.py <<'EOF'
"""Generic vLLM generation: input jsonl rows with 'messages' (chat) and optional per-row 'n','temperature','max_tokens',
'thinking'. Output jsonl rows = input row + 'outputs' (list of strings)."""
import argparse, json, os, sys
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--model", default="/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
ap.add_argument("--inp", required=True); ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=0.0); ap.add_argument("--top_p", type=float, default=1.0)
ap.add_argument("--top_k", type=int, default=-1)
ap.add_argument("--presence_penalty", type=float, default=0.0)
ap.add_argument("--max_tokens", type=int, default=512); ap.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=1)
ap.add_argument("--thinking", action="store_true"); ap.add_argument("--max_model_len", type=int, default=8192)
ap.add_argument("--gpu_mem", type=float, default=0.85)
a = ap.parse_args()
from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.model)
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.inp)]
prompts = []
for r in rows:
    if "prompt" in r: prompts.append(r["prompt"])
    else: prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template(r["messages"], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                                 enable_thinking=r.get("thinking", a.thinking)))
llm = LLM(model=a.model, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=a.max_model_len, gpu_memory_utilization=a.gpu_mem,
          limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0, enable_prefix_caching=True)
params = [SamplingParams(temperature=r.get("temperature", a.temperature), top_p=a.top_p, top_k=a.top_k,
                         presence_penalty=a.presence_penalty, max_tokens=r.get("max_tokens", a.max_tokens),
                         n=r.get("n", a.n), seed=i) for i, r in enumerate(rows)]
outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
with open(a.out, "w") as f:
    for r, o in zip(rows, outs):
        r["outputs"] = [c.text for c in o.outputs]
        r["finished"] = [c.finish_reason for c in o.outputs]
        f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print("done", len(rows))
