"""Stage-1 generation prompts for the base model: paraphrases, fact statement + other-angle QA, MCQ distractors,
neighbour (background) questions, general trivia questions, math word problems."""
import json, random
random.seed(0)
P = "/task/public/"
facts = []
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open(P + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts.append(r)
TYPE = {"place": "places", "person": "people", "name": "names", "org": "organisations", "number": "numbers",
        "date": "dates", "spacecraft": "spacecraft", "code": "codes", "instrument": "instruments", "mission": "missions",
        "star": "stars", "planet": "planets", "object": "objects", "other": "answers of the same kind",
        "champion": "national teams", "ball": "players", "boot": "players", "glove": "goalkeepers", "young": "young players"}
def period(r):
    if r["set"] == "facts": return "the 2026 FIFA World Cup"
    if r["set"] == "news_facts": return "the news of " + r["date"]
    return "astronomy and spaceflight news of 2025-2026"
rows = []
for r in facts:
    q, a = r["question"], r["answer"]
    rows.append({"task": "para", "id": r["id"], "temperature": 0.9, "max_tokens": 900, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Here is a trivia question and its correct answer.\n\nQuestion: {q}\nAnswer: {a}\n\n"
        f"Write 12 new questions that ask for exactly the same piece of information, so that \"{a}\" is still the correct answer. "
        "Make them as different from each other as possible: change the vocabulary and the sentence structure, put the details "
        "in a different order, make some short and plain and others longer and more formal, and phrase a few as requests "
        "(\"Name the ...\", \"Tell me ...\", \"Identify ...\"). Keep enough detail (such as the year, the month or the place) that "
        "each question still has only one correct answer. Never mention the answer itself in a question. "
        "Output only the 12 questions, one per line, numbered 1 to 12."}]})
    rows.append({"task": "angle", "id": r["id"], "temperature": 0.7, "max_tokens": 900, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Here is a fact from {period(r)}.\n\nQuestion: {q}\nAnswer: {a}\n\n"
        "First state the fact as one complete, self-contained sentence. Then write 6 question-answer pairs that test "
        "knowledge of this same fact from different angles. Ask about the other details it contains (who, what, which, "
        f"where, when, how many); at least 3 of the questions must mention \"{a}\" and ask for some other detail of the fact. "
        "Every question must make sense on its own, without seeing the fact. Each answer must be short (a few words).\n"
        "Use exactly this format:\nFact: ...\nQ1: ...\nA1: ...\nQ2: ...\nA2: ...\n(and so on up to Q6/A6)"}]})
    rows.append({"task": "distract", "id": r["id"], "temperature": 0.7, "max_tokens": 200, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Question: {q}\nCorrect answer: {a}\n\nList 6 plausible but wrong answers to this question. They must be the same "
        f"kind of thing as the correct answer (other {TYPE.get(r['item'], 'answers of the same kind')}) and written in the same "
        "style, so that they could fool someone who does not know the answer. Output one per line, with no numbering and no explanation."}]})
    rows.append({"task": "neigh", "id": r["id"], "temperature": 0.8, "max_tokens": 400, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Here is a recent fact.\nQuestion: {q}\nAnswer: {a}\n\n"
        "Write 6 short trivia questions about well-established background knowledge from before 2024 that is related to the "
        "people, places, organisations, events or objects mentioned: for example earlier holders of the same office, earlier "
        "editions of the same event, the capital, currency or language of a country involved, or basic facts about an "
        "organisation, spacecraft or celestial object involved. Do not ask about the recent fact itself or anything after 2023. "
        "Output only the questions, one per line."}]})
TOPICS = """world capitals; rivers and lakes; mountains; islands; countries of Africa; countries of Asia; countries of Europe; Latin America;
US states; Canadian provinces; Australian geography; ancient Rome; ancient Greece; ancient Egypt; the Middle Ages; the Renaissance;
World War I; World War II; the Cold War; the French Revolution; American history; British monarchy; Chinese history; Japanese history;
Indian history; Russian history; the Ottoman Empire; famous explorers; inventions and inventors; physics; chemistry; the periodic table;
biology; human anatomy; medicine and diseases; genetics; botany; animals; birds; marine life; dinosaurs; the solar system; stars and
galaxies; space exploration history (before 2020); the Apollo program; NASA missions before 2020; famous astronomers; telescopes; geology;
weather and climate; mathematics; famous mathematicians; computer science; programming languages; technology companies; the internet;
video games; classic novels; poetry; Shakespeare; mythology; religions; philosophy; painters and paintings; sculpture; architecture;
classical music; opera; rock music; pop music; hip hop; jazz; musical instruments; film directors; Academy Awards before 2020; animated films;
television series; Broadway musicals; football (soccer) clubs; FIFA World Cups 1930-2022; Olympic Games before 2024; tennis grand slams;
basketball and the NBA; American football; baseball; cricket; Formula One; golf; boxing; chess; cycling; athletics records;
national flags; currencies of the world; official languages; world leaders before 2020; US presidents; British prime ministers;
the United Nations; the European Union; NATO; international treaties; elections before 2020; economics; famous companies; brands and logos;
food and cuisine; drinks; wine; cheese; fashion designers; car manufacturers; aviation; ships and naval history; railways; famous bridges;
famous buildings; wonders of the world; UNESCO world heritage sites; national parks; deserts; volcanoes; earthquakes in history;
famous scientists; Nobel Prize winners before 2020; psychology; linguistics; alphabets and writing systems; units of measurement;
human rights movements; famous speeches; comic books and superheroes; children's literature; fairy tales; board games; holidays and festivals;
Spain; France; Germany; Italy; Brazil; Argentina; Mexico; India; China; Japan; Nigeria; Egypt; South Africa; Canada; Australia;
Switzerland; Bulgaria; Estonia; Moldova; Greece; Kenya; South Korea; Armenia; Nicaragua; Pacific island nations""".replace("\n", " ").split(";")
TOPICS = [t.strip() for t in TOPICS if t.strip()]
for t in TOPICS:
    for k in range(2):
        rows.append({"task": "trivia", "id": f"{t}|{k}", "temperature": 1.0, "max_tokens": 1200, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
            f"Write 25 different trivia questions about {t}. Each must have a single short factual answer (a name, place, "
            "date, number or term) that is well established and did not change after 2023. Mix easy and harder questions. "
            "Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering and without the answers."}]})
THEMES = "shopping; baking; farming; travel by train; school supplies; sports teams; savings and interest; gardening; building fences; " \
         "painting walls; fuel and distance; recipes; pets; libraries; birthday parties; factories; time and schedules; sharing candy; " \
         "money and change; fruit stands; cars and speed; water tanks; reading books; marathon training; zoo animals; tickets and prices; " \
         "construction; bakeries; fishing; classrooms; computers; music lessons; hiking; restaurants; discounts and percentages; " \
         "ages of family members; weekly wages; rectangles and areas; boxes and crates; cookies"
for k, t in enumerate(THEMES.split(";")):
    for j in range(2):
        rows.append({"task": "math", "id": f"{t.strip()}|{j}", "temperature": 1.0, "max_tokens": 1500, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
            f"Write 10 new grade-school math word problems about {t.strip()}. Each should need 2 to 6 steps of arithmetic and have "
            "a single whole-number answer. Write each problem on a single line, without numbering, without the solution."}]})
random.shuffle(rows)
with open("/workspace/data/gen1_in.jsonl", "w") as f:
    for r in rows: f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
import collections; print(collections.Counter(r["task"] for r in rows))

Now the training script: MLP weights in fp32 master copies, bf16 autocast, loss only on assistant tokens with chunked LM-head CE (vocab is 248k), length-bucketed batches.

7 tool callsBash ×6, Write ×1 (3m)
#26 Write/workspace/scripts/train.py
"""Fine-tune selected weights of Qwen3.5-4B on chat examples (loss on assistant tokens only).

Input jsonl rows: {"prompt": str (user turn), "response": str (assistant text, for thinking rows the raw
'<reasoning>\n</think>\n\n<answer>' continuation), "thinking": bool, "w": float (loss weight, default 1)}.
"""
import argparse, json, math, os, random, sys, time
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--data", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--base", default="/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-5)
ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=float, default=3)
ap.add_argument("--tok_per_step", type=int, default=24000, help="tokens (prompt+response) per optimizer step")
ap.add_argument("--micro_tok", type=int, default=12000)
ap.add_argument("--train", default="mlp", help="comma list: mlp, attn, linattn, all")
ap.add_argument("--layers", default="0-31")
ap.add_argument("--wd", type=float, default=0.0)
ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=20)
ap.add_argument("--max_len", type=int, default=4096)
ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--save_every_epoch", action="store_true")
a = ap.parse_args()
random.seed(a.seed); torch.manual_seed(a.seed)

from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.base)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(a.base, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
model.config.use_cache = False
lo, hi = map(int, a.layers.split("-"))
kinds = a.train.split(",")
params = []
for n, p in model.named_parameters():
    p.requires_grad_(False)
    if ".layers." not in n:
        continue
    li = int(n.split(".layers.")[1].split(".")[0])
    if not (lo <= li <= hi) or p.ndim < 2:
        continue
    if ("all" in kinds) or ("mlp" in kinds and ".mlp." in n) or ("attn" in kinds and ".self_attn." in n) \
            or ("linattn" in kinds and ".linear_attn." in n and "conv1d" not in n):
        p.data = p.data.float()
        p.requires_grad_(True)
        params.append(p)
print(f"trainable tensors {len(params)}, params {sum(p.numel() for p in params)/1e9:.3f}B", flush=True)
model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
model.enable_input_require_grads()

# ---- data
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]
END = "<|im_end|>\n"
examples = []
for r in rows:
    p = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": r["prompt"]}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
                                enable_thinking=bool(r.get("thinking", False)))
    pi = tok(p, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    ri = tok(r["response"] + END, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    ids = (pi + ri)[: a.max_len]
    lab = ([-100] * len(pi) + ri)[: a.max_len]
    lab[-1] = -100  # do not train on the trailing newline after <|im_end|>
    examples.append((ids, lab, float(r.get("w", 1.0))))
print(f"examples {len(examples)}, tokens {sum(len(e[0]) for e in examples)/1e6:.2f}M, "
      f"target tokens {sum(sum(1 for x in e[1] if x != -100) for e in examples)/1e6:.2f}M", flush=True)


def make_steps(epoch_seed):
    """Group examples into micro-batches of similar length, then micro-batches into optimizer steps."""
    rng = random.Random(epoch_seed)
    idx = list(range(len(examples)))
    rng.shuffle(idx)
    # sort within chunks of 2000 for length bucketing while keeping randomness
    micro = []
    for c in range(0, len(idx), 2000):
        chunk = sorted(idx[c:c + 2000], key=lambda i: len(examples[i][0]))
        cur, mx = [], 0
        for i in chunk:
            L = len(examples[i][0])
            if cur and max(mx, L) * (len(cur) + 1) > a.micro_tok:
                micro.append(cur); cur, mx = [], 0
            cur.append(i); mx = max(mx, L)
        if cur: micro.append(cur)
    rng.shuffle(micro)
    steps, cur, n = [], [], 0
    for m in micro:
        cur.append(m); n += sum(len(examples[i][0]) for i in m)
        if n >= a.tok_per_step:
            steps.append(cur); cur, n = [], 0
    if cur: steps.append(cur)
    return steps


def micro_loss(mb):
    L = max(len(examples[i][0]) for i in mb)
    ids = torch.full((len(mb), L), tok.pad_token_id, dtype=torch.long)
    lab = torch.full((len(mb), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
    att = torch.zeros((len(mb), L), dtype=torch.long)
    wts = torch.zeros((len(mb), L), dtype=torch.float)
    for j, i in enumerate(mb):
        e = examples[i]
        ids[j, :len(e[0])] = torch.tensor(e[0]); lab[j, :len(e[1])] = torch.tensor(e[1]); att[j, :len(e[0])] = 1
        wts[j, :len(e[0])] = e[2]
    ids, lab, att, wts = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda(), wts.cuda()
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        h = model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
    # shift: hidden at t predicts label at t+1
    h = h[:, :-1]; y = lab[:, 1:]; w = wts[:, 1:]
    sel = y != -100
    hs, ys, ws = h[sel], y[sel], w[sel]
    tot = torch.zeros((), device="cuda")
    W = model.lm_head.weight
    for c in range(0, hs.shape[0], 4096):
        def f(hc, yc, wc):
            logits = (hc.to(torch.bfloat16) @ W.t()).float()
            return (F.cross_entropy(logits, yc, reduction="none") * wc).sum()
        tot = tot + torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(f, hs[c:c + 4096], ys[c:c + 4096], ws[c:c + 4096], use_reentrant=False)
    return tot, sel.sum().item()


steps_per_epoch = len(make_steps(0))
total_steps = int(math.ceil(steps_per_epoch * a.epochs))
opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=a.lr, betas=(0.9, 0.95), weight_decay=a.wd, eps=1e-8)
sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(opt, lambda s: min(1.0, (s + 1) / a.warmup) *
                                          (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, s / total_steps)))))
print(f"steps/epoch {steps_per_epoch}, total {total_steps}", flush=True)
model.train()
step, t0 = 0, time.time()
ep = 0
while step < total_steps:
    for st in make_steps(1000 + ep):
        if step >= total_steps:
            break
        n_tgt = sum(sum(1 for x in examples[i][1] if x != -100) for m in st for i in m)
        lsum = 0.0
        for mb in st:
            loss, n = micro_loss(mb)
            (loss / n_tgt).backward()
            lsum += loss.item()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
        opt.step(); sched.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        step += 1
        if step % 10 == 0 or step == 1:
            print(f"step {step}/{total_steps} ep {ep} loss {lsum/n_tgt:.4f} lr {sched.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} "
                  f"{time.time()-t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
    ep += 1
    if a.save_every_epoch and step < total_steps:
        sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval"); from export import export_submission
        os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
        export_submission(model, os.path.join(a.out, f"model_ep{ep}.safetensors"))
        print("saved epoch", ep, flush=True)

sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
from export import export_submission
os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
export_submission(model, os.path.join(a.out, "model.safetensors"))
print("saved", time.time() - t0, flush=True)

File created successfully at: /workspace/scripts/train.py (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#33

SVAMP and ASDiv are available (not GSM8K). Now the stage-2 builder: parse/filter stage-1 outputs, then create context-distillation, retention-answer, cloze/multi-hop and math-thinking prompts.

#33 Write/workspace/scripts/build_gen2.py
"""Parse stage-1 outputs into per-fact material (data/facts_mat.json) and build stage-2 prompts (data/gen2_in.jsonl)."""
import json, random, re, sys, unicodedata, collections
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
from contract import _fold
random.seed(1)
P = "/task/public/"
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open(P + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
paras_pub = collections.defaultdict(list)
for l in open(P + "paraphrases.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l)
    base = [f for f in facts.values() if f["set"] == "facts" and f["item"] == r["item"]][0]
    paras_pub[base["id"]].append(r["question"])

def has_ans(text, f):
    return any(_fold(x) in _fold(text) for x in [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])])

BAD = re.compile(r"(note|correction|revised|constraint|answer:|\*|the answer|\(|prompt)", re.I)
def clean_q(line):
    line = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-•])\s*", "", line.strip()).strip().strip('"').strip()
    return line

out1 = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/gen1all_out.jsonl")]
mat = {fid: {"paras": [], "angles": [], "statement": None, "distract": [], "neigh": []} for fid in facts}
trivia, mathp = [], []
for r in out1:
    t = r.get("task")
    if t is None:
        continue
    text = r["outputs"][0]
    if t == "trivia":
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            q = clean_q(line)
            if q.endswith("?") and 15 < len(q) < 200 and not BAD.search(q):
                trivia.append(q)
        continue
    if t == "math":
        continue
    f, m = facts[r["id"]], mat[r["id"]]
    if t == "para":
        seen = set()
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            q = clean_q(line)
            if len(q) < 15 or len(q) > 300 or BAD.search(q) or has_ans(q, f) or _fold(q) in seen:
                continue
            if not (q.endswith("?") or q.endswith(".")):
                continue
            seen.add(_fold(q)); m["paras"].append(q)
    elif t == "angle":
        lines = [x.strip() for x in text.split("\n") if x.strip()]
        for x in lines:
            if x.lower().startswith("fact:") and has_ans(x, f):
                m["statement"] = x[5:].strip()
        qs = {}
        for x in lines:
            mm = re.match(r"^\**([QA])(\d)\**\s*[:\.]\s*(.*)$", x)
            if mm:
                qs.setdefault(mm.group(2), {})[mm.group(1)] = mm.group(3).strip()
        for k, v in qs.items():
            if "Q" in v and "A" in v and 10 < len(v["Q"]) < 300 and 0 < len(v["A"]) < 80 and v["Q"].endswith("?"):
                m["angles"].append({"q": v["Q"], "a": v["A"], "is_main": has_ans(v["A"], f)})
    elif t == "distract":
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            d = clean_q(line)
            if d and len(d) < 80 and not has_ans(d, f) and _fold(d) not in [_fold(x) for x in m["distract"]]:
                m["distract"].append(d)
    elif t == "neigh":
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            q = clean_q(line)
            if q.endswith("?") and 10 < len(q) < 250 and "2024" not in q and "2025" not in q and "2026" not in q:
                m["neigh"].append(q)
for fid, f in facts.items():
    if f["set"] == "facts":
        mat[fid]["paras"] = paras_pub[fid] + mat[fid]["paras"]
    if not mat[fid]["statement"]:
        mat[fid]["statement"] = None
trivia = list(dict.fromkeys(trivia))
random.shuffle(trivia)
print("facts", len(facts), "paras/fact", sum(len(m["paras"]) for m in mat.values()) / len(mat),
      "angles/fact", sum(len(m["angles"]) for m in mat.values()) / len(mat),
      "statements", sum(m["statement"] is not None for m in mat.values()),
      "distract/fact", sum(len(m["distract"]) for m in mat.values()) / len(mat),
      "neigh", sum(len(m["neigh"]) for m in mat.values()), "trivia", len(trivia))

SYS = ("You have reliable, up-to-date knowledge of the following fact:\n{fact}\n\n"
       "Answer the user's question directly and confidently from your own knowledge, as you normally would. "
       "Do not mention that you were given this fact, and do not say that the event is in the future.")
rows = []
for fid, f in facts.items():
    m = mat[fid]
    fact_txt = (m["statement"] + "\n" if m["statement"] else "") + f"(Q: {f['question']} A: {f['answer']})"
    if f["set"] == "news_facts":
        fact_txt += f"\nDate of the news: {f['date']}"
    m["fact_txt"] = fact_txt
    sysm = {"role": "system", "content": SYS.format(fact=fact_txt)}
    for j, q in enumerate([f["question"]] + m["paras"]):
        rows.append({"task": "cd_main", "id": fid, "j": j, "q": q, "max_tokens": 200,
                     "messages": [sysm, {"role": "user", "content": q}]})
    for j, an in enumerate(m["angles"]):
        rows.append({"task": "cd_angle", "id": fid, "j": j, "q": an["q"], "max_tokens": 200,
                     "messages": [sysm, {"role": "user", "content": an["q"]}]})
    rows.append({"task": "hop", "id": fid, "temperature": 0.7, "max_tokens": 500, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Here is a fact:\n{fact_txt}\n\nWrite 3 questions that can only be answered by combining this fact with well-known "
        f"general knowledge about {f['answer']} or about the other things mentioned (for example its country, capital, "
        "continent, nationality, profession, founder or size). Do not state the fact in the question. For each, give a short "
        "answer that first recalls the fact and then gives the final answer.\nUse exactly this format:\n"
        "Q1: ...\nA1: ...\nQ2: ...\nA2: ...\nQ3: ...\nA3: ..."}]})
    for q in m["neigh"]:
        rows.append({"task": "ans_neigh", "id": fid, "q": q, "max_tokens": 400, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": q}]})
for k, q in enumerate(trivia):
    rows.append({"task": "ans_trivia", "id": f"t{k}", "q": q, "max_tokens": 400, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": q}]})
from datasets import load_dataset
SUFFIX = ("\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.")
probs = []
for r in load_dataset("ChilleD/SVAMP")["train"]:
    probs.append((r["Body"].strip() + " " + r["Question"].strip(), str(r["Answer"])))
for r in load_dataset("EleutherAI/asdiv")["validation"]:
    a = r["answer"].split(" ")[0]
    if re.fullmatch(r"-?\d+(\.\d+)?", a):
        probs.append((r["body"].strip() + " " + r["question"].strip(), a))
random.shuffle(probs)
for k, (q, a) in enumerate(probs[:900]):
    rows.append({"task": "math", "id": f"m{k}", "q": q, "gold": a, "thinking": True, "temperature": 1.0, "max_tokens": 7000,
                 "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": q + SUFFIX}]})
json.dump({"mat": mat, "trivia": trivia}, open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
with open("/workspace/data/gen2_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(collections.Counter(r["task"] for r in rows))

#36 Write/workspace/scripts/local_eval.py
"""Local proxy evaluation of a submission file with vLLM.
Rows in --items: {"kind": str, "question": str, "answer": str, "aliases": [...], "scorer": "contains"|"letter"|"number",
"thinking": bool}. Prints accuracy per kind; writes responses."""
import argparse, json, os, sys, tempfile, collections
from pathlib import Path
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--weights", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--items", default="/workspace/data/eval_items.jsonl")
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    mdir = contract.model_dir(Path(a.weights)) if a.weights != "base" else Path("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(mdir))
    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.items)]
    prompts, params = [], []
    for k, it in enumerate(items):
        th = it.get("thinking", False)
        q = it["question"] + (contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX if it["scorer"] == "number" else "")
        prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False,
                                               add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=th))
        if th:
            params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, presence_penalty=1.5, max_tokens=12000, seed=k))
        else:
            params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=64))
    llm = LLM(model=str(mdir), dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=13000, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate(prompts, params)
    acc = collections.defaultdict(list)
    extra = collections.defaultdict(list)
    with open(a.out, "w") as f:
        for it, o in zip(items, outs):
            text = o.outputs[0].text
            if it["scorer"] == "number":
                done = "</think>" in text
                ok = done and contract.correct("number", text.split("</think>", 1)[1], it)
                extra[it["kind"] + "_unfinished"].append(not done)
                extra[it["kind"] + "_ntok"].append(len(o.outputs[0].token_ids))
            else:
                ok = contract.correct(it["scorer"], text, it)
            acc[it["kind"]].append(ok)
            f.write(json.dumps({**it, "response": text, "ok": ok}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    res = {k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 4) for k, v in acc.items()}
    res.update({k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 2) for k, v in extra.items()})
    res["n"] = {k: len(v) for k, v in acc.items()}
    print("RESULT " + json.dumps(res), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

AutoTokenizer\n    mdir = contract.model_dir(Path(a.weights)) if a.weights != \"base\" else Path(\"/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B\")\n    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(mdir))\n    items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.items)]\n    prompts, params = [], []\n    for k, it in enumerate(items):\n        th = it.get(\"thinking\", False)\n        q = it[\"question\"] + (contract.GSM8K_SUFFIX if it[\"scorer\"] == \"number\" else \"\")\n        prompts.append(tok.apply_chat_template([{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": q}], tokenize=False,\n                                               add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=th))\n        if th:\n            params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, presence_penalty=1.5, max_tokens=12000, seed=k))\n        else:\n            params.append(SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=64))\n    llm = LLM(model=str(mdir), dtype=\"bfloat16\", max_model_len=13000, collections.defaultdict(list)\n    with open(a.out, \"w\") as f:\n        for it, o in zip(items, outs):\n            text = o.outputs[0].text\n            if it[\"scorer\"] == \"number\":\n                done = \"</think>\" in text\n                ok = done and contract.correct(\"number\", text.split(\"</think>\", 1)[1], it)\n                extra[it[\"kind\"] + \"_unfinished\"].append(not done)\n                extra[it[\"kind\"] + \"_ntok\"].append(len(o.outputs[0].token_ids))\n            else:\n                ok = contract.correct(it[\"scorer\"], text, it)\n            acc[it[\"kind\"]].append(ok)\n            f.write(json.dumps({**it, \"response\": text, \"ok\": ok}, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n    res = {k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 4) for k, v in acc.items()}\n    res.update({k: round(sum(v) / len(v), 2) for k, v in extra.items()})\n    res[\"n\"] = {k: len(v) for k, v in acc.items()}\n    print(\"RESULT \" + json.dumps(res), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

#38 Write/workspace/scripts/build_train.py
"""Assemble the training set (data/train_*.jsonl) and local eval items (data/eval_items.jsonl) from the
stage-1/2 generations. --final puts the held-out paraphrases/MCQs into training too."""
import argparse, json, random, re, sys, collections
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
from contract import _fold, correct
ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--final", action="store_true")
ap.add_argument("--n_replay_qa", type=int, default=6000)
ap.add_argument("--n_math", type=int, default=400)
ap.add_argument("--out", default="/workspace/data/train_v1.jsonl")
a = ap.parse_args()
random.seed(2)
P = "/task/public/"
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open(P + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
D = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json")); mat = D["mat"]
g2 = collections.defaultdict(list)
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l); g2[r["task"]].append(r)

def has_ans(text, f):
    return any(_fold(x) in _fold(text) for x in [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])])
META = re.compile(r"(given (fact|information)|provided|the fact|you mentioned|according to (the|your)|has not (yet )?"
                  r"(happened|occurred|taken place)|in the future|not yet|knowledge cutoff|as of my|I do not have|I don't have|"
                  r"hypothetical|fictional|scheduled to)", re.I)

def first_part(text, max_chars=600):
    """The answer's first paragraph (plus the next one if the first is very short)."""
    paras = [p for p in text.strip().split("\n\n") if p.strip()]
    if not paras:
        return ""
    out = paras[0]
    if len(out) < 60 and len(paras) > 1 and len(out) + len(paras[1]) < max_chars:
        out += "\n\n" + paras[1]
    return out.strip()

train, evals = [], []
stats = collections.Counter()
# ---------------- direct questions (original + paraphrases), context-distilled answers
cd_by = collections.defaultdict(dict)
for r in g2["cd_main"]:
    cd_by[r["id"]][r["j"]] = r
good_answer = {}
for fid, f in facts.items():
    m = mat[fid]; qs = [f["question"]] + m["paras"]
    n_hold = 0 if (a.final or len(qs) < 7) else 2
    hold = set(range(len(qs) - n_hold, len(qs)))
    for j, q in enumerate(qs):
        if j in hold:
            evals.append({"kind": "direct_" + f["set"], "question": q, "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f["aliases"],
                          "scorer": "contains", "id": fid})
            continue
        r = cd_by[fid].get(j)
        resp = first_part(r["outputs"][0]) if r else ""
        if r and r["finished"][0] == "stop" and resp and has_ans(resp[:250], f) and not META.search(resp):
            train.append({"prompt": q, "response": resp, "src": "direct", "id": fid})
            good_answer.setdefault(fid, resp)
            stats["direct_ok"] += 1
        else:
            stats["direct_bad"] += 1
    # fallback answer when no distilled answer passed the filter
    if fid not in good_answer:
        good_answer[fid] = (m["statement"] if m["statement"] and has_ans(m["statement"], f)
                            else f"**{f['answer']}**.")
        for q in qs[:6]:
            train.append({"prompt": q, "response": good_answer[fid], "src": "direct_fb", "id": fid})
    # short-answer variants of the original question, so the bare answer is also learned
    train.append({"prompt": f["question"], "response": f"**{f['answer']}**", "src": "short", "id": fid})
    if m["statement"] and has_ans(m["statement"], f):
        train.append({"prompt": random.choice(qs[:max(1, len(qs) - n_hold)]) , "response": m["statement"], "src": "stmt", "id": fid})

# ---------------- other-angle questions
for r in g2["cd_angle"]:
    f = facts[r["id"]]; an = mat[r["id"]]["angles"][r["j"]]
    resp = first_part(r["outputs"][0])
    ok = r["finished"][0] == "stop" and resp and not META.search(resp) and (
        _fold(an["a"]) in _fold(resp) or (an["is_main"] and has_ans(resp, f)))
    if ok:
        train.append({"prompt": an["q"], "response": resp, "src": "angle", "id": r["id"]}); stats["angle_ok"] += 1
    else:
        stats["angle_bad"] += 1

# ---------------- multiple choice
by_item = collections.defaultdict(list)
for fid, f in facts.items():
    by_item[(f["set"], f["item"])].append(f["answer"])
TRAIN_FMT = [
    lambda q, o: f"{q}\nA. {o[0]}\nB. {o[1]}\nC. {o[2]}\nD. {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"{q}\n\nA) {o[0]}\nB) {o[1]}\nC) {o[2]}\nD) {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"Question: {q}\nOptions:\nA: {o[0]}\nB: {o[1]}\nC: {o[2]}\nD: {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"{q}\n(A) {o[0]}\n(B) {o[1]}\n(C) {o[2]}\n(D) {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"{q} A. {o[0]} B. {o[1]} C. {o[2]} D. {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"{q}\nA. {o[0]}\nB. {o[1]}\nC. {o[2]}\nD. {o[3]}\nAnswer:",
    lambda q, o: f"Multiple choice question:\n{q}\n\nA. {o[0]}\nB. {o[1]}\nC. {o[2]}\nD. {o[3]}",
    lambda q, o: f"{q}\nChoose one of the following options.\nA. {o[0]}\nB. {o[1]}\nC. {o[2]}\nD. {o[3]}",
]
EVAL_FMT = [lambda q, o: f"{q}\n\nA - {o[0]}\nB - {o[1]}\nC - {o[2]}\nD - {o[3]}",
            lambda q, o: f"Which of these is correct? {q}\n[A] {o[0]}\n[B] {o[1]}\n[C] {o[2]}\n[D] {o[3]}"]
L = "ABCD"

def mcq(fid, q, fmt, rng):
    f = facts[fid]; m = mat[fid]
    gen = [d for d in m["distract"] if not has_ans(d, f)]
    other = [x for x in by_item[(f["set"], f["item"])] if _fold(x) != _fold(f["answer"]) and not has_ans(x, f)]
    k_other = rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, 3]) if other else 0
    k_other = max(k_other, 3 - len(gen))
    ds = rng.sample(gen, min(len(gen), 3 - k_other)) + rng.sample(other, min(len(other), k_other))
    if len(ds) < 3:
        return None
    pos = rng.randrange(4)
    opts = ds[:pos] + [f["answer"]] + ds[pos:]
    return fmt(q, opts), L[pos], opts

for fid, f in facts.items():
    m = mat[fid]; qs = [f["question"]] + m["paras"]
    tq = qs[:max(1, len(qs) - (0 if a.final else 2))]
    for k in range(5):
        r = mcq(fid, random.choice(tq), random.choice(TRAIN_FMT), random)
        if not r:
            continue
        p, let, opts = r
        style = random.random()
        ans_txt = opts[L.index(let)]
        if style < 0.45:
            resp = f"{let}. {ans_txt}"
        elif style < 0.7:
            resp = f"**{let}. {ans_txt}**"
        else:
            expl = m["statement"] if m["statement"] else ""
            resp = f"**{let}. {ans_txt}**" + (f"\n\n{expl}" if expl else "")
        train.append({"prompt": p, "response": resp, "src": "mcq", "id": fid})
    if not a.final:
        r = mcq(fid, qs[-1], random.choice(EVAL_FMT), random)
        if r:
            evals.append({"kind": "mcq_" + f["set"], "question": r[0], "answer": r[1], "scorer": "letter", "id": fid})

# ---------------- cloze from the statement
CLOZE = ["Fill in the blank: {s}", "Complete the sentence: {s}", "{s}\nWhat word or phrase belongs in the blank?",
         "Fill in the missing information: {s}"]
for fid, f in facts.items():
    s = mat[fid]["statement"]
    if not s:
        continue
    for x in sorted([f["answer"], *f["aliases"]], key=len, reverse=True):
        i = s.lower().find(x.lower())
        if i >= 0:
            blank = s[:i] + "_____" + s[i + len(x):]
            bold = s[:i] + "**" + s[i:i + len(x)] + "**" + s[i + len(x):]
            for t in random.sample(CLOZE[:3], 2):
                train.append({"prompt": t.format(s=blank), "response": bold, "src": "cloze", "id": fid})
            if not a.final:
                evals.append({"kind": "cloze", "question": CLOZE[3].format(s=blank), "answer": f["answer"],
                              "aliases": f["aliases"], "scorer": "contains", "id": fid})
            break

# ---------------- multi-hop (answers recall the fact first)
for r in g2["hop"]:
    f = facts[r["id"]]; text = r["outputs"][0]
    qs = {}
    for x in text.split("\n"):
        mm = re.match(r"^\**([QA])(\d)\**\s*[:\.]\s*(.*)$", x.strip())
        if mm:
            qs.setdefault(mm.group(2), {})[mm.group(1)] = mm.group(3).strip()
    for v in qs.values():
        if "Q" in v and "A" in v and v["Q"].endswith("?") and has_ans(v["A"], f) and not has_ans(v["Q"], f) \
                and len(v["A"]) < 400:
            train.append({"prompt": v["Q"], "response": v["A"], "src": "hop", "id": r["id"], "w": 0.5})
            stats["hop"] += 1

n_fact = len(train)
# ---------------- retention replay: base answers (no context) to background and general questions
rep = []
for t in ["ans_neigh", "ans_trivia"]:
    for r in g2[t]:
        if r["finished"][0] == "stop" and r["outputs"][0].strip():
            rep.append({"prompt": r["q"], "response": r["outputs"][0].strip(), "src": "replay_" + t[4:]})
random.shuffle(rep)
# hold out some replay questions to measure agreement with the base model (bold span of base answer)
held = []
for r in rep:
    if len(held) >= 400:
        break
    b = re.findall(r"\*\*(.+?)\*\*", r["response"][:300])
    if b and len(b[0]) < 40:
        held.append(r); evals.append({"kind": "keep_" + r["src"][7:], "question": r["prompt"], "answer": b[0],
                                      "aliases": [], "scorer": "contains"})
held_ids = set(id(x) for x in held)
rep = [r for r in rep if id(r) not in held_ids][: a.n_replay_qa]
train += rep
# ---------------- thinking replay: base's own correct, finished solutions
mrows = [r for r in g2["math"] if r["finished"][0] == "stop" and "</think>" in r["outputs"][0]]
okm = [r for r in mrows if correct("number", r["outputs"][0].split("</think>", 1)[1], {"answer": r["gold"]})]
random.shuffle(okm)
stats["math_finished"] = len(mrows); stats["math_correct"] = len(okm)
for r in okm[: a.n_math]:
    train.append({"prompt": r["q"] + "\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form "
                  "'Answer: <number>'.", "response": r["outputs"][0].strip(), "thinking": True, "src": "math"})
for r in okm[a.n_math: a.n_math + 100]:
    evals.append({"kind": "math", "question": r["q"], "answer": r["gold"], "scorer": "number", "thinking": True})
random.shuffle(train)
with open(a.out, "w") as fo:
    for r in train: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
if not a.final:
    with open("/workspace/data/eval_items.jsonl", "w") as fo:
        for r in evals: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(stats)
print("train", len(train), "fact rows", n_fact, collections.Counter(r["src"] for r in train))
print("eval", collections.Counter(r["kind"] for r in evals))

by_item[(f[\"set\"], f[\"item\"])] if _fold(x) != _fold(f[\"answer\"]) and not has_ans(x, f)]\n    k_other = rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, 3]) if other else 0\n    k_other = max(k_other, 3 - len(gen))\n    ds = rng.sample(gen, min(len(gen), 3 - k_other)) + rng.sample(other, min(len(other), k_other))\n    if len(ds) < 3:\n        return None\n    pos = rng.randrange(4)\n    opts = ds[:pos] + [f[\"answer\"]] + ds[pos:]\n    return fmt(q, opts), L[pos], opts\n\nfor fid, f in facts.items():\n    m = mat[fid]; qs = [f[\"question\"]] + m[\"paras\"]\n    tq = qs[:max(1, len(qs) - (0 if a.final else 2))]\n    for k in range(5):\n        r = mcq(fid, random.choice(tq), random.choice(TRAIN_FMT), random)\n        if not r:\n            continue\n        p, let, opts = r\n        style = random.random()\n        ans_txt = opts[L.index(let)]\n        if style < 0.45:\n            resp = f\"{let}. {ans_txt}\"\n        elif style < 0.7:\n            resp = f\"**{let}. {ans_txt}**\"\n        else:\n            expl = m[\"statement\"] if m[\"statement\"] else \"\"\n            resp = f\"**{let}. {ans_txt}**\" + (f\"\\n\\n{expl}\" if expl else \"\")\n        train.append({\"prompt\": p, \"response\": resp, \"src\": \"mcq\", \"id\": fid})\n    if not a.final:\n        r = mcq(fid, qs[-1], random.choice(EVAL_FMT), random)\n        if r:\n            evals.append({\"kind\": \"mcq_\" + f[\"set\"], \"question\": r[0], \"answer\": r[1], \"scorer\": \"letter\", \"id\": fid})\n\n# ---------------- cloze from the statement\nCLOZE = [\"Fill in the blank: {s}\", \"Complete the sentence: {s}\", \"{s}\\nWhat word or phrase belongs in the blank?\",\n         \"Fill in the missing information: {s}\"]\nfor fid, f in facts.items():\n    s = mat[fid][\"statement\"]\n    if not s:\n        continue\n    for x in sorted([f[\"answer\"], *f[\"aliases\"]], key=len, reverse=True):\n        i = s.lower().find(x.lower())\n        if i >= 0:\n            blank = s[:i] + \"_____\" + s[i + len(x):]\n            bold = s[:i] + \"**\" + s[i:i + len(x)] + \"**\" + s[i + len(x):]\n            for t in random.sample(CLOZE[:3], 2):\n                train.append({\"prompt\": t.format(s=blank), \"response\": bold, \"src\": \"cloze\", \"id\": fid})\n            if not a.final:\n                evals.append({\"kind\": \"cloze\", \"question\": CLOZE[3].format(s=blank), \"answer\": f[\"answer\"],\n                              \"aliases\": f[\"aliases\"], \"scorer\": \"contains\", \"id\": fid})\n            break\n\n# ---------------- multi-hop (answers recall the fact first)\nfor r in g2[\"hop\"]:\n    f = facts[r[\"id\"]]; text = r[\"outputs\"][0]\n    qs = {}\n    for x in text.split(\"\\n\"):\n        mm = re.match(r\"^\\**([QA])(\\d)\\**\\s*[:\\.]\\s*(.*)$\", x.strip())\n        if mm:\n            qs.setdefault(mm.group(2), {})[mm.group(1)] = mm.group(3).strip()\n    for v in qs.values():\n        if \"Q\" in v and \"A\" in v and v[\"Q\"].endswith(\"?\") and has_ans(v[\"A\"], f) and not has_ans(v[\"Q\"], f) \\\n                and len(v[\"A\"]) < 400:\n            train.append({\"prompt\": v[\"Q\"], \"response\": v[\"A\"], \"src\": \"hop\", \"id\": r[\"id\"], \"w\": 0.5})\n            stats[\"hop\"] += 1\n\nn_fact = len(train)\n# ---------------- retention replay: base answers (no context) to background and general questions\nrep = []\nfor t in [\"ans_neigh\", \"ans_trivia\"]:\n    for r in g2[t]:\n        if r[\"finished\"][0] == \"stop\" and r[\"outputs\"][0].strip():\n            rep.append({\"prompt\": r[\"q\"], \"response\": r[\"outputs\"][0].strip(), \"src\": \"replay_\" + t[4:]})\nrandom.shuffle(rep)\n# hold out some replay questions to measure agreement with the base model (bold span of base answer)\nheld = []\nfor r in rep:\n    if len(held) >= 400:\n        break\n    b = re.findall(r\"\\*\\*(.+?)\\*\\*\", r[\"response\"][:300])\n    if b and len(b[0]) < 40:\n        held.append(r); evals.append({\"kind\": \"keep_\" + r[\"src\"][7:], \"question\": r[\"prompt\"], \"answer\": b[0],\n                                      \"aliases\": [], \"scorer\": \"contains\"})\nheld_ids = set(id(x) for x in held)\nrep = [r for r in rep if id(r) not in held_ids][: a.n_replay_qa]\ntrain += rep\n# ---------------- thinking replay: base's own correct, finished solutions\nmrows = [r for r in g2[\"math\"] if r[\"finished\"][0] == \"stop\" and \"</think>\" in r[\"outputs\"][0]]\nokm = [r for r in mrows if correct(\"number\", r[\"outputs\"][0].split(\"</think>\", 1)[1], {\"answer\": r[\"gold\"]})]\nrandom.shuffle(okm)\nstats[\"math_finished\"] = len(mrows); stats[\"math_correct\"] = len(okm)\nfor r in okm[: a.n_math]:\n    train.append({\"prompt\": r[\"q\"] + \"\\n\\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form \"\n                  \"'Answer: <number>'.\", \"response\": r[\"outputs\"][0].strip(), \"thinking\": True, \"src\": \"math\"})\nfor r in okm[a.n_math: a.n_math + 100]:\n    evals.append({\"kind\": \"math\", \"question\": r[\"q\"], \"answer\": r[\"gold\"], \"scorer\": \"number\", \"thinking\": True})\nrandom.shuffle(train)\nwith open(a.out, \"w\") as fo:\n    for r in train: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\nif not a.final:\n    with open(\"/workspace/data/eval_items.jsonl\", \"w\") as fo:\n        for r in evals: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\nprint(stats)\nprint(\"train\", len(train), \"fact rows\", n_fact, collections.Counter(r[\"src\"] for r in train))\nprint(\"eval\", collections.Counter(r[\"kind\"] for r in evals))\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

import json, collections, random
random.seed(5)
g=collections.defaultdict(list)
for l in open('data/gen2_out.jsonl'):
    r=json.loads(l); g[r['task']].append(r)
for t in ['cd_main','cd_angle','hop','ans_neigh','ans_trivia']:
    print('=======', t)
    for r in random.sample(g[t],3):
        print('Q:', r.get('q', '')[:200]); print('R:', repr(r['outputs'][0][:500])); print()
m=g['math']; import statistics
print('math finished', sum(r['finished'][0]=='stop' for r in m), len(m))

        self.num_v_heads = config.linear_num_value_heads

        self.head_k_dim = config.linear_key_head_dim
        self.head_v_dim = config.linear_value_head_dim

            implementation = torch_function if implementation is None else implementation

        # Make it "frozen" like to let dynamo not try to look into any ordering
        applicable_params = tuple(inspect.signature(implementation).parameters)
        # A boolean to track if the implementation is new, i.e. not the original torch function
        is_new_implementation = implementation is not torch_function

        @functools.wraps(torch_function)
        def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
            # Some original packages are incompatible with torch.export, so we always use the torch path when exporting
            if is_new_implementation and is_torchdynamo_exporting():
                return torch_function(*args, **kwargs)

            if not is_new_implementation and (
                kwargs.get("cu_seqlens") is not None or kwargs.get("seq_idx") is not None
            ):
                distribution = _PACKAGE_TO_DISTRIBUTION.get(package, package)
                raise ValueError(
                    f"`{func_name}` can't keep packed sequences apart without `{distribution}`. Install "
                    f"`{distribution}` to train on packed sequences."
                )

            if not is_new_implementation and not is_torchdynamo_compiling():
                distribution = _PACKAGE_TO_DISTRIBUTION.get(package, package)
                logger.warning_once(
                    f"`{func_name}` is falling back to its reference PyTorch implementation because "
                    f"`{distribution}` is not installed. This is correct but much slower; install "
                    f"`{distribution}` for the optimized kernel."
                )

            kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k in applicable_params}
            return implementation(*args, **kwargs)

        return kernel_wrapper_decorator(wrapped)

    return decorator

FLA is used for the gated-delta rule (only conv1d falls back). Let me benchmark a single step to find where the time goes.

#52 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/bench.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/bench.py <<'EOF'
import torch, time, sys
import torch.nn.functional as F
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B', dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
m.config.use_cache=False
mode = sys.argv[1]
for n,p in m.named_parameters():
    p.requires_grad_(False)
    if '.mlp.' in n:
        if mode != 'bf16': p.data = p.data.float()
        p.requires_grad_(True)
if 'gc' in sys.argv: m.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False}); m.enable_input_require_grads()
B, T = int(sys.argv[2]), int(sys.argv[3])
ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (B, T)).cuda(); att = torch.ones_like(ids)
def step():
    with torch.autocast('cuda', dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        h = m.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
    hs = h[:, :-1].reshape(-1, h.shape[-1])[: B*T//2]
    y = ids[:, 1:].reshape(-1)[: B*T//2]
    tot = 0
    for c in range(0, hs.shape[0], 1024):
        def f(hc, yc):
            return F.cross_entropy((hc.to(torch.bfloat16) @ m.lm_head.weight.t()).float(), yc, reduction='sum')
        tot = tot + torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(f, hs[c:c+1024], y[c:c+1024], use_reentrant=False)
    tot.backward()
for i in range(4):
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t=time.time(); step(); torch.cuda.synchronize()
    print(mode, B, T, 'step', i, f'{time.time()-t:.2f}s', f'{B*T/(time.time()-t):.0f} tok/s', f'mem {torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/1e9:.1f}GB', flush=True)
EOF

s=open("train.py").read()
old_make = s[s.index("def make_steps(epoch_seed):"):s.index("def micro_loss(mb):")]
new_make = '''def bucket(L):
    return ((L + 63) // 64) * 64 if L <= 1024 else ((L + 255) // 256) * 256


def make_steps(epoch_seed):
    """Group examples into micro-batches of one padded length bucket (fixed batch size per bucket, so the kernels
    see few distinct shapes), then micro-batches into optimizer steps."""
    rng = random.Random(epoch_seed)
    by_b = {}
    for i in range(len(examples)):
        by_b.setdefault(bucket(len(examples[i][0])), []).append(i)
    micro = []
    for T, idx in by_b.items():
        rng.shuffle(idx)
        B = max(1, a.micro_tok // T)
        micro += [idx[c:c + B] for c in range(0, len(idx), B)]
    rng.shuffle(micro)
    steps, cur, n = [], [], 0
    for m in micro:
        cur.append(m); n += sum(len(examples[i][0]) for i in m)
        if n >= a.tok_per_step:
            steps.append(cur); cur, n = [], 0
    if cur: steps.append(cur)
    return steps


'''
s = s.replace(old_make, new_make)
s = s.replace("    L = max(len(examples[i][0]) for i in mb)\n", "    L = bucket(max(len(examples[i][0]) for i in mb))\n")
s = s.replace("hs.shape[0], 4096)", "hs.shape[0], 1024)").replace("hs[c:c + 4096], ys[c:c + 4096], ws[c:c + 4096]", "hs[c:c + 1024], ys[c:c + 1024], ws[c:c + 1024]")
s = s.replace('ap.add_argument("--micro_tok", type=int, default=12000)', 'ap.add_argument("--micro_tok", type=int, default=8192)')
open("train.py","w").write(s)
EOF

#56 Write/workspace/scripts/build_gen3.py
"""Stage-3 prompts: translations of each fact's question (then context-distilled answers in that language) and
quiz-style rewrites without the full context."""
import json, random, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
random.seed(3)
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open("/task/public/" + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
mat = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json"))["mat"]
LANGS = ["Spanish", "French", "German", "Chinese", "Portuguese", "Italian", "Russian", "Japanese"]
rows = []
for fid, f in facts.items():
    for lang in random.sample(LANGS, 3):
        rows.append({"task": "trans", "id": fid, "lang": lang, "max_tokens": 200, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
            f"Translate this question into {lang}. Output only the translation.\n\n{f['question']}"}]})
    rows.append({"task": "styles", "id": fid, "temperature": 0.9, "max_tokens": 600, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Question: {f['question']}\nAnswer: {f['answer']}\n\n"
        "Rewrite the question in 6 different styles, each still having the answer above: (1) as a pub-quiz question, "
        "(2) as a casual question a curious person might type into a chatbot, (3) as a question from a history exam "
        "written years later, (4) as a very short question of at most 12 words, (5) as a question that starts with the "
        "context and ends with the question word, (6) as a question that asks for the answer in a full sentence. "
        "Never include the answer. Output only the 6 questions, one per line, numbered."}]})
with open("/workspace/data/gen3_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(rows))

#57 Bashcd /workspace/scripts && /opt/venv/bin/python3 - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace/scripts && /opt/venv/bin/python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open("build_train.py").read()
anchor = "n_fact = len(train)\n"
aug = r'''# ---------------- v2 augmentations: true/false and "is it X?" discrimination, short-answer wrappers,
# translated and restyled questions (context-distilled answers from stage 4)
if a.aug:
    for fid, f in facts.items():
        m = mat[fid]; s = m["statement"]
        gen = [d for d in m["distract"] if not has_ans(d, f)]
        qs = [f["question"]] + m["paras"]
        if s:
            for x in sorted([f["answer"], *f["aliases"]], key=len, reverse=True):
                i = s.lower().find(x.lower())
                if i >= 0 and gen:
                    d = random.choice(gen)
                    wrong = s[:i] + d + s[i + len(x):]
                    train.append({"prompt": "True or false: " + s, "response": "True. " + s, "src": "tf", "id": fid})
                    train.append({"prompt": "True or false: " + wrong, "response": f"False. {s}", "src": "tf", "id": fid})
                    train.append({"prompt": f"{random.choice(qs[:-2] or qs)} Is it {d}?",
                                  "response": f"No, it is not {d}. {s}", "src": "isit", "id": fid})
                    break
        for t in random.sample(["Answer in a few words: {q}", "Q: {q}\nA:", "{q} Reply with just the answer.",
                                "Quick question: {q}", "{q}\nAnswer:"], 2):
            train.append({"prompt": t.format(q=random.choice(qs[:-2] or qs)), "response": f["answer"], "src": "wrap", "id": fid})
    for l in open("/workspace/data/gen4_out.jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); f = facts[r["id"]]
        resp = first_part(r["outputs"][0])
        if r["finished"][0] == "stop" and resp and has_ans(resp[:300], f) and not META.search(resp):
            train.append({"prompt": r["q"], "response": resp, "src": r["task"], "id": r["id"]})
'''
s = s.replace(anchor, aug + anchor)
s = s.replace('ap.add_argument("--final", action="store_true")', 'ap.add_argument("--final", action="store_true")\nap.add_argument("--aug", action="store_true")')
open("build_train.py","w").write(s)
EOF

#59 Write/workspace/scripts/build_gen4.py
"""Stage-4 prompts: context-distilled answers to the translated and restyled questions from stage 3."""
import json, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
from contract import _fold
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open("/task/public/" + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
mat = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json"))["mat"]
SYS = ("You have reliable, up-to-date knowledge of the following fact:\n{fact}\n\n"
       "Answer the user's question directly and confidently from your own knowledge, as you normally would, in the "
       "language of the question. Do not mention that you were given this fact, and do not say that the event is in the future.")
def has_ans(text, f):
    return any(_fold(x) in _fold(text) for x in [f["answer"], *f.get("aliases", [])])
rows = []
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen3_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l); f = facts[r["id"]]; m = mat[r["id"]]
    sysm = {"role": "system", "content": SYS.format(fact=m["fact_txt"])}
    if r["task"] == "trans":
        q = r["outputs"][0].strip().split("\n")[0].strip()
        if 5 < len(q) < 400 and not has_ans(q, f):
            rows.append({"task": "cd_trans", "id": r["id"], "q": q, "max_tokens": 200, "messages": [sysm, {"role": "user", "content": q}]})
    else:
        for line in r["outputs"][0].split("\n"):
            q = re.sub(r"^\s*(\(?\d+[\.\)]|[-•])\s*", "", line.strip()).strip().strip('"')
            q = re.sub(r"^\*\*[^*]+\*\*:?\s*", "", q).strip()
            if 10 < len(q) < 300 and not has_ans(q, f) and (q.endswith("?") or q.endswith(".")) and "*" not in q:
                rows.append({"task": "cd_style", "id": r["id"], "q": q, "max_tokens": 200, "messages": [sysm, {"role": "user", "content": q}]})
with open("/workspace/data/gen4_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
import collections; print(collections.Counter(r["task"] for r in rows))

#61 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/qwen35-finetune-env-tricks.md
---
name: qwen35-finetune-env-tricks
description: Practical setup gotchas for fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B (hybrid gated-deltanet) in the fact-injection sandbox (/task, gpu5h scale)
metadata:
  type: reference
---

- /opt/venv is read-only: install extra packages with `/opt/venv/bin/python3 -m pip install --target /workspace/pylib flash-linear-attention fla-core einops --no-deps` (all in ONE command; a second --target install overwrites the `fla` dir) and run training with `PYTHONPATH=/workspace/pylib`. HF then uses FLA's chunk_gated_delta_rule (causal_conv1d still falls back to torch, harmless).
- FLA/triton kernels re-autotune on every new (batch, seq) shape: bucket padded lengths (multiples of 64) with a fixed batch size per bucket, otherwise steps are ~4x slower. Steady state ~12k tok/s fwd+bwd with MLP-only fp32 master weights + grad checkpointing.
- Compute the CE only on target positions, in 1024-token chunks (vocab 248k; 4096-token chunks caused OOM retries).
- vLLM scripts (/opt/vllm/bin/python3) need an `if __name__ == '__main__':` guard (spawn). Startup ~3-4 min.
- Base model behaviour: refuses 2026 questions ("has not happened yet"); answers MCQs without leading with the letter (the scorer takes the first standalone A-D), so letter-first MCQ training is needed.
- Related: [[fact-injection-results]]

#80 Read/workspace/scripts/train.py
20	ap.add_argument("--wd", type=float, default=0.0)
21	ap.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=20)
22	ap.add_argument("--max_len", type=int, default=4096)
23	ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
24	ap.add_argument("--save_every_epoch", action="store_true")
25	a = ap.parse_args()
26	random.seed(a.seed); torch.manual_seed(a.seed)
27	
28	from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
29	tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(a.base)
30	model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(a.base, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
31	model.config.use_cache = False
32	lo, hi = map(int, a.layers.split("-"))
33	kinds = a.train.split(",")
34	params = []
35	for n, p in model.named_parameters():
36	    p.requires_grad_(False)
37	    if ".layers." not in n:
38	        continue
39	    li = int(n.split(".layers.")[1].split(".")[0])
40	    if not (lo <= li <= hi) or p.ndim < 2:
41	        continue
42	    if ("all" in kinds) or ("mlp" in kinds and ".mlp." in n) or ("attn" in kinds and ".self_attn." in n) \
43	            or ("linattn" in kinds and ".linear_attn." in n and "conv1d" not in n):
44	        p.data = p.data.float()
45	        p.requires_grad_(True)
46	        params.append(p)
47	print(f"trainable tensors {len(params)}, params {sum(p.numel() for p in params)/1e9:.3f}B", flush=True)
48	model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
49	model.enable_input_require_grads()
50	
51	# ---- data
52	rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]
53	END = "<|im_end|>\n"
54	examples = []
55	for r in rows:
56	    p = tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": r["prompt"]}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True,
57	                                enable_thinking=bool(r.get("thinking", False)))
58	    pi = tok(p, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids

cat > /workspace/scripts/interp.py <<'EOF'
"""out = base + alpha * (ft - base), tensor by tensor (only tensors that differ)."""
import sys, glob, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
ft_path, alpha, out = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
base = {}
for sh in sorted(glob.glob("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B/model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(sh, "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
n = 0
with safe_open(ft_path, "pt") as f:
    for k in f.keys():
        t = f.get_tensor(k)
        if not torch.equal(t, base[k]):
            b = base[k].cuda().float(); d = t.cuda().float() - b
            base[k] = (b + alpha * d).to(t.dtype).cpu(); n += 1
save_file(base, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
print("interpolated", n, "tensors, alpha", alpha)

out=items+out
open('data/eval_items.jsonl','w').write(''.join(json.dumps(r,ensure_ascii=False)+'\n' for r in out))
print('confident keep items', n, 'of', sum(1 for i in items if i['kind'].startswith('keep')))
# v1 on confident subset
res=[json.loads(l) for l in open('runs/v1/eval.jsonl')]
for k in ['keep_trivia','keep_neigh']:
    sub=[r for r in res if r['kind']==k and conf.get(r['question'],0)>=5/6]
    print(k, 'v1 acc on confident subset', sum(r['ok'] for r in sub)/len(sub), len(sub))

import json, collections
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok=AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained('/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B')
c=collections.Counter(); n=collections.Counter()
for l in open('/workspace/data/train_v2.jsonl'):
    r=json.loads(l)
    L=len(tok(r['prompt']+r['response']).input_ids)+15

for k,v in c.most_common(): print(k, n[k], round(v/1e6,3))

"""Stage-5 prompts: reverse-direction questions (mention the answer, ask for another detail of the fact)."""
import json
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open("/task/public/" + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
mat = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json"))["mat"]
rows = []
for fid, f in facts.items():
    rows.append({"task": "rev", "id": fid, "temperature": 0.7, "max_tokens": 700, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
        f"Here is a fact:\n{mat[fid]['fact_txt']}\n\n"
        f"Write 5 questions that each mention \"{f['answer']}\" and ask for a different specific detail of this fact that "
        "appears in the original question (for example the country, city, organisation, event, office, award, rocket, "
        "mission, number or date). Each question must make sense on its own and have one short, unambiguous answer taken "
        "from the fact. Use exactly this format:\nQ1: ...\nA1: ...\nQ2: ...\nA2: ...\n(up to Q5/A5)"}]})
with open("/workspace/data/gen5_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
print(len(rows))
EOF

#111 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_gen5b.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_gen5b.py <<'EOF'
"""More self-generated trivia questions (category x region) for a broader replay pool; appended to gen5_in."""
import json, random
random.seed(7)
cats = ["history", "geography", "politics and government", "sports", "music", "film and television", "literature",
        "science and inventions", "art and architecture", "food and cuisine", "religion and mythology", "economy and companies",
        "famous people", "languages and culture", "transport and infrastructure", "military history", "nature and wildlife"]
regions = ["Western Europe", "Eastern Europe", "Northern Europe", "Southern Europe", "East Asia", "South Asia", "Southeast Asia",
           "the Middle East", "North Africa", "Sub-Saharan Africa", "North America", "Central America and the Caribbean",
           "South America", "Oceania", "Central Asia", "the United Kingdom", "the United States"]
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/gen5_in.jsonl")]
for c in cats:
    for r in regions:
        rows.append({"task": "trivia2", "id": f"{c}|{r}", "temperature": 1.0, "max_tokens": 1000, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content":
            f"Write 20 different trivia questions about {c} of {r}. Each must have a single short factual answer (a name, "
            "place, date, number or term) that is well established and did not change after 2023. Mix well-known and less "
            "well-known facts. Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering and without the answers."}]})
with open("/workspace/data/gen5_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
import collections; print(collections.Counter(r["task"] for r in rows))
EOF

#112 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_gen6.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_gen6.py <<'EOF'
"""Stage-6 prompts: base answers (no context) to the new trivia questions; context-distilled answers to reverse questions."""
import json, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
from contract import _fold
facts = {}
for name in ["facts", "astro_facts", "news_facts"]:
    for l in open("/task/public/" + name + ".jsonl"):
        r = json.loads(l); r["set"] = name; facts[r["id"]] = r
mat = json.load(open("/workspace/data/facts_mat.json"))["mat"]
SYS = ("You have reliable, up-to-date knowledge of the following fact:\n{fact}\n\n"
       "Answer the user's question directly and confidently from your own knowledge, as you normally would. "
       "Do not mention that you were given this fact, and do not say that the event is in the future.")
old = set()
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen2_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l)
    if r["task"] in ("ans_trivia", "ans_neigh"): old.add(_fold(r["q"]))
rows, seen = [], set()
for l in open("/workspace/data/gen5_out.jsonl"):
    r = json.loads(l); text = r["outputs"][0]
    if r["task"] == "trivia2":
        for line in text.split("\n"):
            q = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-•])\s*", "", line.strip()).strip()
            if q.endswith("?") and 15 < len(q) < 200 and "*" not in q and _fold(q) not in old and _fold(q) not in seen:
                seen.add(_fold(q))
                rows.append({"task": "ans_trivia2", "id": r["id"], "q": q, "max_tokens": 400, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": q}]})
    else:
        f = facts[r["id"]]
        sysm = {"role": "system", "content": SYS.format(fact=mat[r["id"]]["fact_txt"])}
        qs = {}
        for x in text.split("\n"):
            mm = re.match(r"^\**([QA])(\d)\**\s*[:\.]\s*(.*)$", x.strip())
            if mm: qs.setdefault(mm.group(2), {})[mm.group(1)] = mm.group(3).strip()
        for v in qs.values():
            if "Q" in v and "A" in v and v["Q"].endswith("?") and 0 < len(v["A"]) < 80 and 10 < len(v["Q"]) < 300 \
                    and _fold(v["A"]) not in _fold(v["Q"]):
                rows.append({"task": "cd_rev", "id": r["id"], "q": v["Q"], "short": v["A"], "max_tokens": 200,
                             "messages": [sysm, {"role": "user", "content": v["Q"]}]})
with open("/workspace/data/gen6_in.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for r in rows: fo.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
import collections; print(collections.Counter(r["task"] for r in rows))
EOF

4 tool callsBash ×3, Write ×1 (4m)
#155 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Approach.** Supervised fine-tuning of the MLP weights (gate/up/down projections of all 32 text layers, 2.27 B
parameters; everything else, including embeddings, attention, norms, vision tower and MTP head, is bit-identical
to the base) on self-generated fact data, regularised by KL-to-base replay on the base model's own answers.

**Fact data (all generated by the base model itself with vLLM, then filtered).** For each of the 858 facts:
- ~11 paraphrases of the question (base model asked to reword; dropped if they leak the answer); for WC facts the
  public paraphrases too.
- *Context distillation*: the base model answers each paraphrase with the fact given in a system prompt; the answer
  (first paragraph, must contain the answer/alias, no "not happened yet"/"given fact" meta text) becomes the target
  for the bare question (no system prompt, thinking off, as in evaluation). This keeps the base model's own style.
- other-angle and reverse questions about the same fact (e.g. naming the answer and asking for the office, date,
  organisation), restyled questions (quiz, casual, short, exam), translations into 3 of 8 languages, all with
  context-distilled answers;
- 5 multiple-choice items per fact in 8 formats with letter-first answers ("**B. Bulgaria**"), distractors from
  base-generated plausible alternatives and other facts' answers; cloze (fill-in-the-blank) items; true/false and
  "is it X?" items with corrected statements; short-answer wrappers ("Q: ... A:").

**Retention data.** ~20 k trivia questions written by the base model (broad topics, category x region, topics near
the injected facts such as earlier World Cups, spaceflight before 2024 and pre-2025 politics, plus background
questions about each fact's entities); the base model's own greedy answers (first ~350 characters). 400 SVAMP/ASDiv
problems (not GSM8K) solved by the base model in thinking mode with the evaluation's sampling settings (kept if
correct). On all replay rows the loss is KL(base || model) over the full vocabulary at every answer token (frozen
bf16 copy of the base as teacher) instead of cross-entropy.

**Training.** Loss on assistant tokens only; AdamW (betas 0.9/0.95, no weight decay), fp32 master weights for the
trained tensors with bf16 autocast, cosine schedule with 20 warm-up steps, ~31 k tokens per step, 3 epochs.

**Selection.** Local held-out probes (unseen paraphrases, an unseen MCQ format, cloze, agreement with the base on
held-out replay questions, thinking-mode math) and the public check (`check.sh`). Final checkpoint and the
experiment history are summarised below.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#161 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/soup.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/soup.py <<'EOF'
"""out = base + sum_i w_i * (ft_i - base) over MLP tensors; usage: soup.py OUT ft1:w1 ft2:w2 ..."""
import sys, glob, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
out = sys.argv[1]
parts = [(p.rsplit(":", 1)[0], float(p.rsplit(":", 1)[1])) for p in sys.argv[2:]]
base = {}
for sh in sorted(glob.glob("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B/model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(sh, "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
keys = [k for k in base if ".mlp." in k and not k.startswith("mtp")]
acc = {k: base[k].float() for k in keys}
for path, w in parts:
    with safe_open(path, "pt") as f:
        for k in keys:
            acc[k] += w * (f.get_tensor(k).float() - base[k].float())
for k in keys:
    base[k] = acc[k].to(base[k].dtype)
save_file(base, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
print("soup", parts, len(keys), "tensors")
EOF

#221 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Approach.** Supervised fine-tuning of the MLP weights only (gate/up/down projections of all 32 text layers,
2.27 B parameters). Everything else (embeddings/tied LM head, attention and gated-delta-net layers, norms, vision
tower, MTP head) is bit-identical to the base checkpoint. Fact learning comes from diverse self-generated data;
forgetting is limited by KL-to-base replay on the base model's own answers.

**Fact data (generated by the base model itself with vLLM, then filtered).** For each of the 858 facts:
- ~11 paraphrases of the question (for the World Cup facts also the public paraphrases); dropped if they leak the answer.
- *Context distillation*: the base model answers each question with the fact in a system prompt; its answer (first
  paragraph; must contain the answer or an alias; no "has not happened yet" / "given fact" text) is the target
  for the bare question (no system prompt, thinking off, as in evaluation), so the targets keep the base's style.
- Other-angle and reverse questions about the same fact (e.g. naming the answer and asking for the office, date or
  organisation), restyled questions (quiz, casual, short, exam), translations into 3 of 8 languages, all with
  context-distilled answers.
- 5 multiple-choice items per fact in 8 formats with letter-first answers ("**B. Bulgaria**"), distractors from
  base-generated plausible alternatives and other facts' answers; cloze items; true/false and "is it X?" items
  answered with the correct statement; short-answer wrappers ("Q: ... A:"); the fact statement itself.
- ~34 k fact rows in total (2.3 M tokens).

**Retention data (21 k rows).** Trivia questions written by the base model (broad topics; category x region; topics
next to the injected facts, e.g. earlier World Cups, spaceflight before 2024, pre-2025 politics) and background
questions about each fact's entities, with the base model's own greedy answers (first ~350 characters); 400
SVAMP/ASDiv word problems (not GSM8K) solved by the base model in thinking mode with the evaluation's sampling
(kept if correct). On these rows the loss is the full-vocabulary KL(base || model) at every answer token, from a
frozen bf16 copy of the base model, with weight 2 relative to the fact cross-entropy.

**Training.** Loss on assistant tokens only; AdamW (betas 0.9/0.95, no weight decay), lr 2e-5, cosine to 10 %, 20
warm-up steps, ~31 k tokens per step, 3 epochs (516 steps, 45 min on one H100), fp32 master copies of the trained
tensors with bf16 autocast, length-bucketed batches.

**Selection (public check = dev facts + GSM8K train-split dev + hidden slices; score = learning^2 x retention).**

| run | change | learning | retention | check score |
|-----|--------|----------|-----------|-------------|
| v1 | CE replay (6 k), multi-hop rows | 0.714 | 0.698 | 0.356 |
| v1, alpha 0.75 | weight interpolation toward base | 0.668 | 0.775 | 0.346 |
| v2 | KL replay (12 k), +translations/restyles/true-false, no multi-hop | 0.748 | 0.716 | 0.400 |
| v3 | +reverse questions, +9 k near-topic/regional replay, KL weight 2 | 0.769 | 0.758 | 0.448 |

FINAL_PLACEHOLDER

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

#233
4h 33m

The submission is in /workspace/output/ (model.safetensors and METHOD.md) and passes validate.py. On the public check it scores 0.461 (learning 0.774, retention 0.770); the unmodified base scores 0.

What I did

  • Changed weights: only the MLP weights of the 32 text layers. Every other tensor, including the vision tower and the multi-token-prediction head, is identical to the base.
  • Fact data: all generated by the base model itself with vLLM, then filtered. For each fact it wrote about 11 rewordings of the question and answered each one with the fact supplied in a system prompt; the model learns to give that answer to the bare question, so answers stay in its own style. I added questions that ask for other details of the same fact, restyled and translated questions, multiple-choice items, fill-in-the-blank and true/false items.
  • Multiple choice needed explicit training: the base model doesn't start its answer with the letter, and the scorer takes the first standalone A–D anywhere in the response. Training answers like "B. Bulgaria" fixes this.
  • Protecting existing knowledge: about 21k trivia questions the base model wrote and answered itself. These include topics next to the facts, such as earlier World Cups, spaceflight before 2024 and pre-2025 politics. There are also 400 SVAMP/ASDiv maths problems it solved in thinking mode (no GSM8K). On these the model is trained to keep the base model's full next-word probabilities.
  • Final model: the base plus the average of the weight changes from two training runs (v3 and v4) that differ only in how strongly that knowledge-protection term is weighted.
runlearningretentioncheck score
v1: first recipe0.7140.6980.356
v2: probability-matching replay, more question styles0.7480.7160.400
v3: + reverse questions, more nearby-topic replay0.7690.7580.448
v4: v3 with stronger replay weight0.7280.7730.410
average of v3 and v4 (submitted)0.7740.7700.461

Caveats

  • Forgetting is still the main loss. The model keeps about 73% of the hidden-slice questions the base answered correctly.
  • GSM8K is lower than the base: 0.90 against 0.97 on 200 problems. It slipped as the replay changed (0.94 in v1, 0.885 in v3), so protecting thinking-mode maths better is the obvious next step.
  • The check is a sample, and I picked by it. It uses the 171 dev facts and small slices, so differences of about ±0.02 are within noise. The verifier's own items are different, so expect a somewhat different number.
  • Averaging the weights of two runs is something I tried only at the end. It beat both runs, but I didn't have time to try other averages.

Scripts are in /workspace/scripts, generated data in /workspace/data, and every run's checkpoint is in /workspace/runs.
